"""<br>宏观图构建器 - 从微观路网络构建宏观图<br>产出：<br>f"{file_name}_macro_graph.pkl"<br>"""<br>import numpy as np<br>import pandas as pd<br>import pickle<br>import json<br>import matplotlib.pyplot as plt<br>import networkx as nx<br>from collections import defaultdict, deque<br>import os<br>from typing import Dict, List, Tuple, Optional, Any<br># 设置中文字体<br>plt.rcParams['font.family'] = ['simhei']<br>plt.rcParams['axes.unicode_minus'] = False<br>class MacroGraphBuilder:<br>"""宏观图构建器"""<br>def __init__(self):<br>"""初始化构建器"""<br>self.micro_graph = None # 微观图（NetworkX 图）<br>self.macro_graph = None # 宏观图（NetworkX 图）<br>self.micro_to_macro_mapping = {} # 微观点到宏观点的映射<br>self.macro_edges_paths = {} # 宏观边对应的微观路径<br>self.visibility_data = None # 视景数据<br>self.geometry_data = None # 园林几何数据<br>self.manual_macro_nodes = {} # 手动添加的宏观节点 {node_id: node_data}<br>self.manual_macro_edges = {} # 手动添加的宏观边 {(node1, node2): edge_data}<br>def load_data(self, file_name: str):<br><!-- End of picture text -->

111 

<mark>"""加载所有必需的数据""" # 加载视景分析数据 from visibility_analysis_2 import VisibilityDataAnalyzer analyzer = VisibilityDataAnalyzer() # 视景数据路径前缀 visibility_data_prefix = f"视景数据/{file_name}_visibility_analysis" original_excel = f"shujv/4-{file_name}数据坐标.xlsx" network_excel = f"{file_name}-路网络.xlsx" print("=== 加载视景分析数据 ===") if not analyzer.load_visibility_data(visibility_data_prefix): print("视景数据加载失败") return False print("=== 加载网络数据 ===") if not analyzer.load_network_data(original_excel, network_excel): print("网络数据加载失败") return False self.visibility_data = analyzer self.geometry_data = analyzer.geometry_data # 构建微观图 self._build_micro_graph() return True def add_manual_macro_node(self, node_id: int, pos: Tuple[float, float], degree: int = 1, temporal_anomaly: float = 0, spatial_anomaly: float = 0): """手动添加宏观节点 Args: node_id: 节点 ID pos: 节点位置 (x, y) degree: 节点度数 temporal_anomaly: 时间异景程度 spatial_anomaly: 空间异景程度 """ self.manual_macro_nodes[node_id] = { 'pos': pos, 'degree': degree, 'temporal_anomaly': temporal_anomaly, 'spatial_anomaly': spatial_anomaly } print(f"已添加手动宏观节点: {node_id} at {pos}") def add_manual_macro_edge(self, node1: int, node2: int, path: List[int], length: float, turning_points: int = 0, temporal_anomaly_sum: float = 0, spatial_anomaly_sum: float = 0, micro_point_count: int = 0): """手动添加宏观边 Args: node1, node2: 边的两个端点 path: 微观路径 length: 路径长度 turning_points: 转折点数量 temporal_anomaly_sum: 时间异景程度总和 spatial_anomaly_sum: 空间异景程度总和 micro_point_count: 微观点数量 """ edge_key = (min(node1, node2), max(node1, node2)) self.manual_macro_edges[edge_key] = { 'path': path, 'length': length,</mark> 

112 

<mark>'turning_points': turning_points, 'temporal_anomaly_sum': temporal_anomaly_sum, 'spatial_anomaly_sum': spatial_anomaly_sum, 'micro_point_count': micro_point_count } # 同时存储路径映射 self.macro_edges_paths[edge_key] = path print(f"已添加手动宏观边: {node1}-{node2}, 路径长度: {length}") def _add_manual_macro_elements(self): """添加手动指定的宏观节点和边""" # 添加手动宏观节点 for node_id, node_data in self.manual_macro_nodes.items(): if not self.macro_graph.has_node(node_id): self.macro_graph.add_node(node_id, **node_data) print(f"已添加手动宏观节点: {node_id}") else: print(f"节点 {node_id} 已存在，跳过") # 添加手动宏观边 for (node1, node2), edge_data in self.manual_macro_edges.items(): if not self.macro_graph.has_edge(node1, node2): self.macro_graph.add_edge(node1, node2, **edge_data) print(f"已添加手动宏观边: {node1}-{node2}") else: print(f"边 {node1}-{node2} 已存在，跳过") def _build_micro_graph(self): """构建微观图（NetworkX 图）""" self.micro_graph = nx.Graph() # 添加节点 for i, point in enumerate(self.visibility_data.network_points): self.micro_graph.add_node(i, pos=point) # 添加边 for connection in self.visibility_data.network_connections: self.micro_graph.add_edge(connection[0], connection[1]) print(f"微观图构建完成: {self.micro_graph.number_of_nodes()} 个节点, {self.micro_graph.number_of_edges()} 条边") def _identify_entrance_nodes(self): """识别出入口节点（微观图中度为 1 且在宏观图中的节点，或者手动添加的度为 1 的宏观节点）""" entrance_nodes = [] # 找到微观图中度为 1 的节点 for node in self.micro_graph.nodes(): if self.micro_graph.degree[node] == 1: # 检查该节点是否在宏观图中 if node in self.macro_graph.nodes(): entrance_nodes.append(node) # 2. 找到手动添加的度为 1 的宏观节点 for node_id, node_data in self.manual_macro_nodes.items(): if node_data.get('degree', 0) == 1: # 避免重复添加 if node_id not in entrance_nodes: entrance_nodes.append(node_id) print(f"识别到 {len(entrance_nodes)} 个出入口节点: {entrance_nodes}") return entrance_nodes def build_macro_graph(self): """构建宏观图""" if self.micro_graph is None: print("请先加载数据") return False print("=== 构建宏观图 ===") # 1. 识别宏观点</mark> 

113 

<mark>macro_nodes = self._identify_macro_nodes() print(f"识别到 {len(macro_nodes)} 个宏观点") # 2. 构建宏观图 self.macro_graph = nx.Graph() # 添加自动识别的宏观节点 for node_id in macro_nodes: pos = self.micro_graph.nodes[node_id]['pos'] degree = self.micro_graph.degree[node_id] # 获取异景程度 anomaly_scores = self.visibility_data.anomaly_scores.get(node_id, {}) temporal_anomaly = anomaly_scores.get('temporal_anomaly', 0) spatial_anomaly = anomaly_scores.get('spatial_anomaly', 0) self.macro_graph.add_node(node_id, pos=pos, degree=degree, temporal_anomaly=temporal_anomaly, spatial_anomaly=spatial_anomaly) # 3. 计算自动宏观边 self._build_macro_edges(macro_nodes) # 4. 添加手动指定的宏观节点和边 self._add_manual_macro_elements() # 5. 识别出入口节点 self.entrance_nodes = self._identify_entrance_nodes() print(f"宏观图构建完成: {self.macro_graph.number_of_nodes()} 个节点, {self.macro_graph.number_of_edges()} 条边") print(f"出入口节点: {len(self.entrance_nodes)} 个") return True def _identify_macro_nodes(self): """识别宏观点（度为 1 或度≥3 的点）""" macro_nodes = [] for node in self.micro_graph.nodes(): degree = self.micro_graph.degree[node] if degree == 1 or degree >= 3: macro_nodes.append(node) return macro_nodes def _build_macro_edges(self, macro_nodes): """构建宏观边""" macro_node_set = set(macro_nodes) for start_node in macro_nodes: # 使用 BFS 找到从当前宏观点到其他宏观点的路径 visited = set() queue = deque([(start_node, [start_node])]) visited.add(start_node) while queue: current_node, path = queue.popleft() # 检查相邻节点 for neighbor in self.micro_graph.neighbors(current_node): if neighbor in visited: continue new_path = path + [neighbor] visited.add(neighbor) # 如果到达另一个宏观点，创建宏观边 if neighbor in macro_node_set and neighbor != start_node: if not self.macro_graph.has_edge(start_node, neighbor): self._add_macro_edge(start_node, neighbor, new_path) else: # 继续搜索（只有度为 2 的点才继续） if self.micro_graph.degree[neighbor] == 2: queue.append((neighbor, new_path)) def</mark> <u><mark>_add_macro_edge(self,</mark></u> <mark>start_node, end_node,</mark> <u><mark>path):</mark></u> 

114 

<mark>"""添加宏观边并计算其属性""" # 计算路径长度 path_length = self._calculate_path_length(path) # 计算转折点数量 turning_points = self._count_turning_points(path) # 计算异景程度（不包含端点） temporal_sum, spatial_sum, point_count = self._calculate_anomaly_sums(path[1:-1]) # 添加边 self.macro_graph.add_edge(start_node, end_node, path=path, length=path_length, turning_points=turning_points, temporal_anomaly_sum=temporal_sum, spatial_anomaly_sum=spatial_sum, micro_point_count=point_count) # 保存宏观边对应的微观路径 edge_key = (min(start_node, end_node), max(start_node, end_node)) self.macro_edges_paths[edge_key] = path def _calculate_path_length(self, path): """计算路径长度""" length = 0 for i in range(len(path) - 1): pos1 = self.micro_graph.nodes[path[i]]['pos'] pos2 = self.micro_graph.nodes[path[i+1]]['pos'] length += np.sqrt((pos1[0] - pos2[0])**2 + (pos1[1] - pos2[1])**2) return length def _count_turning_points(self, path, angle_threshold=20): """计算转折点数量（度为 2 且偏转超过阈值的点）""" if len(path) < 3: return 0 turning_count = 0 for i in range(1, len(path) - 1): # 检查当前点的度是否为 2 if self.micro_graph.degree[path[i]] != 2: continue # 计算转向角度 pos_prev = self.micro_graph.nodes[path[i-1]]['pos'] pos_curr = self.micro_graph.nodes[path[i]]['pos'] pos_next = self.micro_graph.nodes[path[i+1]]['pos'] # 计算向量 vec1 = (pos_curr[0] - pos_prev[0], pos_curr[1] - pos_prev[1]) vec2 = (pos_next[0] - pos_curr[0], pos_next[1] - pos_curr[1]) # 计算角度 angle = self._calculate_angle(vec1, vec2) # 如果偏转角度超过阈值，算作转折点 if abs(180 - angle) > angle_threshold: turning_count += 1 return turning_count def _calculate_angle(self, vec1, vec2): """计算两个向量之间的夹角（度）""" dot_product = vec1[0] * vec2[0] + vec1[1] * vec2[1] mag1 = np.sqrt(vec1[0]**2 + vec1[1]**2) mag2 = np.sqrt(vec2[0]**2 + vec2[1]**2) if mag1 == 0 or mag2 == 0: return 0 cos_angle = dot_product / (mag1 * mag2) cos_angle = np.clip(cos_angle, -1, 1) # 防止数值误差 angle_rad = np.arccos(cos_angle) angle_deg = np.degrees(angle_rad) return angle_deg</mark>

<mark>def _calculate_anomaly_sums(self, point_ids): """计算路径上点的异景程度之和""" temporal_sum = 0 spatial_sum = 0 point_count = len(point_ids) for point_id in point_ids: anomaly_scores = self.visibility_data.anomaly_scores.get(point_id, {}) temporal_sum += anomaly_scores.get('temporal_anomaly', 0) spatial_sum += anomaly_scores.get('spatial_anomaly', 0) return temporal_sum, spatial_sum, point_count def save_macro_graph(self, file_name: str): """保存宏观图数据""" # 保存宏观图 macro_graph_data = { 'nodes': dict(self.macro_graph.nodes(data=True)), 'edges': {(u, v): data for u, v, data in self.macro_graph.edges(data=True)}, 'macro_edges_paths': self.macro_edges_paths, 'entrance_nodes': getattr(self, 'entrance_nodes', []) # 保存出入口节点 } with open(f"视景数据/{file_name}_macro_graph.pkl", 'wb') as f: pickle.dump(macro_graph_data, f) # 保存为 JSON（便于查看） json_data = { 'nodes': {str(k): v for k, v in self.macro_graph.nodes(data=True)}, 'edges': {f"{u}-{v}": data for u, v, data in self.macro_graph.edges(data=True)}, 'macro_edges_paths': {f"{k[0]}-{k[1]}": v for k, v in self.macro_edges_paths.items()}, 'entrance_nodes': getattr(self, 'entrance_nodes', []) } with open(f"视景数据/{file_name}_macro_graph.json", 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f: json.dump(json_data, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2) print(f"宏观图已保存: 视景数据/{file_name}_macro_graph.pkl/json") return True def load_macro_graph(self, file_name: str) -> bool: """加载宏观图数据""" try: with open(f"视景数据/{file_name}_macro_graph.pkl", 'rb') as f: macro_graph_data = pickle.load(f) # 重构宏观图 self.macro_graph = nx.Graph() self.macro_graph.add_nodes_from(macro_graph_data['nodes'].items()) self.macro_graph.add_edges_from([(u, v, data) for (u, v), data in macro_graph_data['edges'].items()]) self.macro_edges_paths = macro_graph_data['macro_edges_paths'] # 加载出入口节点 self.entrance_nodes = macro_graph_data.get('entrance_nodes', []) print(f"宏观图已加载: {self.macro_graph.number_of_nodes()} 个节点, {self.macro_graph.number_of_edges()} 条边") print(f"出入口节点: {len(self.entrance_nodes)} 个: {self.entrance_nodes}") return True except Exception as e: print(f"加载宏观图失败: {e}") return False def main(file_name): """主函数""" # 创建宏观图构建器 builder = MacroGraphBuilder() # 加载数据 print("=== 加载数据 ===") if not builder.load_data(file_name):</mark>

multi_objective_path_planner.py<br>"""<br>多目标游园路径规划器 - 基于宏观图的游园路径多目标优化<br>优化：简化的宏观图<br>产出：<br>f"视景数据/{file_name}_simple_macro_graph.pkl"<br>f"视景数据/{file_name}_optimal_paths.pkl"<br>"""<br>import numpy as np<br>import pandas as pd<br>import pickle<br>import json<br>import matplotlib.pyplot as plt<br>import plotly.tools as tls<br>import networkx as nx<br>from collections import defaultdict, deque<br>import os<br>import random<br>from typing import Dict, List, Tuple, Optional, Any, Set<br>import warnings<br>warnings.filterwarnings('ignore')<br># 多目标优化库<br>from deap import base, creator, tools, algorithms<br># 继承宏观图构建器<br>from macro_graph_builder import MacroGraphBuilder<br># 设置中文字体<br>plt.rcParams['font.family'] = ['simhei']<br><!-- End of picture text -->

<mark>class MultiObjectivePathPlanner(MacroGraphBuilder): """多目标游园路径规划器"""</mark> 

<mark>def __init__(self): """初始化规划器""" super().__init__() self.entrance_nodes = [] # 出入口节点列表 self.optimal_paths = [] # 优化得到的路径列表 self.path_evaluations = [] # 路径评估结果 self.normalized_scores = {} # 归一化分数 # 多目标优化参数 self.population_size = 500 # 种群大小 self.generations = 50 # 迭代次数 self.crossover_prob = 0.7 self.mutation_prob = 0.5</mark> 

<mark>def _normalize_anomaly_scores(self):</mark> 

<mark>"""归一化异景程度分数"""</mark> 

<mark>if not self.macro_graph: return # 收集所有节点和边的异景程度 node_temporal = [] node_spatial = [] edge_temporal = [] edge_spatial = [] # 节点异景程度 for node, data in self.macro_graph.nodes(data=True): node_temporal.append(data.get('temporal_anomaly', 0)) node_spatial.append(data.get('spatial_anomaly', 0)) # 边异景程度 for u, v, data in self.macro_graph.edges(data=True): edge_temporal.append(data.get('temporal_anomaly_sum', 0)) edge_spatial.append(data.get('spatial_anomaly_sum', 0)) # 计算归一化参数 self.normalized_scores = { 'node_temporal_max': max(node_temporal) if node_temporal else 1, 'node_spatial_max': max(node_spatial) if node_spatial else 1, 'edge_temporal_max': max(edge_temporal) if edge_temporal else 1, 'edge_spatial_max': max(edge_spatial) if edge_spatial else 1 } print(f"异景程度归一化参数: {self.normalized_scores}") def _calculate_path_anomaly_score(self, path: List[int]) -> float:</mark> 

<mark>""" 计算路径的总异景程度（归一化后） Args: path: 节点路径列表 Returns: float: 总异景程度分数 """ if len(path) < 2:</mark> 

<mark>return 0 total_score = 0 visited_nodes = set() visited_edges = set() # 节点异景程度（不重复计算） for node in path: if node not in visited_nodes: node_data = self.macro_graph.nodes[node]</mark>

<mark>temporal = node_data.get('temporal_anomaly', 0) / self.normalized_scores['node_temporal_max'] spatial = node_data.get('spatial_anomaly', 0) / self.normalized_scores['node_spatial_max'] total_score += temporal + spatial visited_nodes.add(node) # 边异景程度（不重复计算） for i in range(len(path) - 1):</mark> 

<mark>edge = (min(path[i], path[i+1]), max(path[i], path[i+1])) if edge not in visited_edges and self.macro_graph.has_edge(path[i], path[i+1]): edge_data = self.macro_graph.edges[path[i], path[i+1]] temporal = edge_data.get('temporal_anomaly_sum', 0) / self.normalized_scores['edge_temporal_max'] spatial = edge_data.get('spatial_anomaly_sum', 0) / self.normalized_scores['edge_spatial_max'] total_score += temporal + spatial visited_edges.add(edge) return total_score def _calculate_path_repetition_length(self, path: List[int]) -> float: """ 计算路径的重复长度 Args: path: 节点路径列表 Returns: float: 重复路径长度 """ if len(path) < 2: return 0 visited_edges = set() total_length = 0 repetition_length = 0 for i in range(len(path) - 1): if self.macro_graph.has_edge(path[i], path[i+1]): edge_data = self.macro_graph.edges[path[i], path[i+1]] edge_length = edge_data.get('length', 0) total_length += edge_length edge = (min(path[i], path[i+1]), max(path[i], path[i+1])) if edge in visited_edges: repetition_length += edge_length else: visited_edges.add(edge) return repetition_length def _extend_path_randomly(self, base_path: List[int]) -> List[int]: """随机扩展已有路径，保持起点和终点不变""" if len(base_path) < 3: return base_path extended_path = base_path.copy() start_node = base_path[0] end_node = base_path[-1] # 增加多次插入绕行的机会 num_detours = random.randint(1, 3) # 1-3 次绕行 for _ in range(num_detours): if len(extended_path) > 100: # 防止路径过长 break # 在中间某个位置插入绕行 insert_pos = random.randint(1, len(extended_path) - 2) current_node = extended_path[insert_pos]</mark> 

119 

<mark># 进行更长距离的随机游走 detour = [current_node] for step in range(random.randint(3, 12)): # 从 2-5 增加到 3-12 neighbors = list(self.macro_graph.neighbors(current_node)) if neighbors: # 避免立即返回，但允许更远的探索 if len(detour) > 2: neighbors = [n for n in neighbors if n != detour[-2]] if neighbors: # 增加随机性，有时选择远离目标的路径 if random.random() < 0.3: # 30%概率随机选择 current_node = random.choice(neighbors) else: # 选择度数较高的节点（可能有更多探索机会） neighbors_with_degree = [(n, self.macro_graph.degree[n]) for n in neighbors] neighbors_with_degree.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True) current_node = neighbors_with_degree[0][0] detour.append(current_node) # 将绕行插入原路径 extended_path[insert_pos:insert_pos+1] = detour return extended_path def _generate_prior_paths(self) -> List[List[int]]: """生成先验路径（节点序列格式）""" # 保持原函数不变，返回节点序列 # 在 create_individual 中通过_encode_path 转换 prior_paths = [] for i, start_entrance in enumerate(self.entrance_nodes): for j, end_entrance in enumerate(self.entrance_nodes): if i != j: try: shortest_path = nx.shortest_path(self.macro_graph, start_entrance, end_entrance) if len(shortest_path) >= 2: prior_paths.append(shortest_path) except nx.NetworkXNoPath: continue # 生成更长路径 try: all_simple_paths = list(nx.all_simple_paths( self.macro_graph, start_entrance, end_entrance, cutoff=20)) if all_simple_paths: paths_by_length = {} for path in all_simple_paths: length = len(path) if length not in paths_by_length or len(paths_by_length) < 3: paths_by_length[length] = path for path in paths_by_length.values(): if path not in prior_paths: prior_paths.append(path) except: continue print(f"生成了 {len(prior_paths)} 条先验路径") # 存储先验路径供 create_individual 使用 self.prior_paths = prior_paths return prior_paths def _decode_individual(self, individual: List) -> List[int]: """将步骤选择编码解码为节点路径""" if len(individual) < 3:</mark>

return []<br>start_node, end_node, step_choices = individual[0], individual[1], individual[2]<br>path = [start_node]<br>current_node = start_node<br>for choice_idx in step_choices:<br>neighbors = list(self.macro_graph.neighbors(current_node))<br>if not neighbors:<br>break<br># 确保选择索引有效<br>actual_choice = choice_idx % len(neighbors)<br>next_node = neighbors[actual_choice]<br>path.append(next_node)<br>current_node = next_node<br>if current_node == end_node:<br>break<br># 如果未到达终点，尝试连接到终点<br>if path[-1] != end_node:<br>try:<br>bridge = nx.shortest_path(self.macro_graph, path[-1], end_node)[1:]<br>path.extend(bridge)<br>except nx.NetworkXNoPath:<br>pass<br>return path<br>def _encode_path(self, path: List[int]) -> List:<br>"""将节点路径编码为步骤选择"""<br>if len(path) < 2:<br>return [path[0], path[0], []]<br>start_node, end_node = path[0], path[-1]<br>step_choices = []<br>for i in range(len(path) - 1):<br>current_node = path[i]<br>next_node = path[i + 1]<br>neighbors = list(self.macro_graph.neighbors(current_node))<br>if next_node in neighbors:<br>choice_idx = neighbors.index(next_node)<br>step_choices.append(choice_idx)<br>else:<br># 路径不连通，无法编码<br>step_choices.append(0) # 默认选择第一个邻居<br>return [start_node, end_node, step_choices]<br>def _setup_deap_environment(self):<br>"""设置 DEAP 多目标优化环境"""<br># 创建适应度类（最大化异景程度，最小化重复长度）<br>creator.create("FitnessMulti", base.Fitness, weights=(1.0, -1.0))<br>creator.create("Individual", list, fitness=creator.FitnessMulti)<br>toolbox = base.Toolbox()<br># 注册个体生成函数<br>def create_individual():<br>"""生成基于步骤选择的个体编码"""<br># 降低先验路径使用比例，增加随机探索<br>if random.random() < 0.4 and hasattr(self, 'prior_paths') and self.prior_paths:<br># 使用先验路径，需要编码转换<br>base_path = random.choice(self.prior_paths).copy()<br># 扩展路径（可选）<br>if random.random() < 0.8 and len(base_path) < 50:<br>extended_path = self._extend_path_randomly(base_path)<br>encoded = self._encode_path(extended_path)<br>else:<br>encoded = self._encode_path(base_path)<br>return creator.Individual(encoded)<br><!-- End of picture text -->

1)<br>step_choices.append(choice_idx)<br>current_node = available_neighbors[choice_idx]<br>visited_recently.append(current_node)<br>if current_node == end_node:<br>break<br>return creator.Individual([start_node, end_node, step_choices])<br>toolbox.register("individual", create_individual)<br>toolbox.register("population", tools.initRepeat, list, toolbox.individual)<br># 注册评估函数<br>def evaluate(individual):<br>"""评估基于步骤选择编码的个体"""<br>path = self._decode_individual(individual)<br>if len(path) < 2:<br>return (0, float('inf'))<br>anomaly_score = self._calculate_path_anomaly_score(path)<br>repetition_length = self._calculate_path_repetition_length(path)<br>return (anomaly_score, repetition_length)<br>toolbox.register("evaluate", evaluate)<br># 注册遗传算子<br>def custom_crossover(ind1, ind2):<br>"""基于步骤选择的安全交叉"""<br>if len(ind1) < 3 or len(ind2) < 3:<br>return ind1, ind2<br>steps1, steps2 = ind1[2], ind2[2]<br>if len(steps1) > 2 and len(steps2) > 2:<br># 交换步骤选择序列的中间段<br>cross_point1 = random.randint(1, len(steps1) - 1)<br>cross_point2 = random.randint(1, len(steps2) - 1)<br>new_steps1 = steps1[:cross_point1] + steps2[cross_point2:]<br>new_steps2 = steps2[:cross_point2] + steps1[cross_point1:]<br>ind1[2] = new_steps1<br>ind2[2] = new_steps2<br>return ind1, ind2<br>def custom_mutation(individual):<br><!-- End of picture text -->

"""基于步骤选择的安全变异"""<br>if len(individual) < 3:<br>return individual,<br>steps = individual[2]<br>mutation_type = random.random()<br>if mutation_type < 0.4: # 单点变异<br>if steps:<br>pos = random.randint(0, len(steps) - 1)<br># 随机改变一个选择<br>steps[pos] = random.randint(0, 5) # 假设最多 6 个邻居<br>elif mutation_type < 0.7: # 插入新步骤<br>if len(steps) < 100: # 限制长度<br>pos = random.randint(0, len(steps))<br>steps.insert(pos, random.randint(0, 5))<br>else: # 删除步骤<br>if len(steps) > 2:<br>pos = random.randint(0, len(steps) - 1)<br>steps.pop(pos)<br>return individual,<br>toolbox.register("mate", custom_crossover)<br>toolbox.register("mutate", custom_mutation)<br>#toolbox.register("select", tools.selNSGA2)<br># 使用更多样化的选择策略<br>def diverse_selection(individuals, k):<br>"""结合 NSGA2 和多样性保持的选择策略"""<br>if len(individuals) <= k:<br>return individuals<br># 先用 NSGA2 选择<br>selected = tools.selNSGA2(individuals, min(k, len(individuals) * 3 // 4))<br># 再用基于距离的多样性选择补充<br>if len(selected) < k:<br>remaining = [ind for ind in individuals if ind not in selected]<br>if remaining:<br># 计算拥挤度距离，选择多样化的个体<br>diverse_selected = tools.selNSGA2(remaining, k - len(selected))<br>selected.extend(diverse_selected)<br>return selected[:k]<br>toolbox.register("select", diverse_selection)<br>return toolbox<br>def optimize_paths(self) -> bool:<br>"""执行多目标路径优化"""<br># 检查出入口节点是否已加载<br>if not hasattr(self, 'entrance_nodes') or not self.entrance_nodes:<br>print("出入口节点未加载，请检查宏观图数据")<br>return False<br>print("=== 开始多目标路径优化 ===")<br># 归一化异景程度分数<br>self._normalize_anomaly_scores()<br># 生成先验路径<br>self._generate_prior_paths()<br># 设置 DEAP 环境<br>toolbox = self._setup_deap_environment()<br># 创建初始种群<br>population = toolbox.population(n=self.population_size)<br># 执行多目标优化<br>print(f"运行 NSGA-II 算法: {self.generations}代, 种群大小{self.population_size}")<br># 评估初始种群<br>fitnesses = toolbox.map(toolbox.evaluate, population)<br>for ind, fit in zip(population, fitnesses):<br>ind.fitness.values = fit<br><!-- End of picture text -->

# 进化过程<br>for generation in range(self.generations):<br># 选择<br>offspring = toolbox.select(population, len(population))<br>offspring = list(map(toolbox.clone, offspring))<br># 交叉和变异<br>for child1, child2 in zip(offspring[::2], offspring[1::2]):<br>if random.random() < self.crossover_prob:<br>toolbox.mate(child1, child2)<br>del child1.fitness.values<br>del child2.fitness.values<br>for mutant in offspring:<br>if random.random() < self.mutation_prob:<br>toolbox.mutate(mutant)<br>del mutant.fitness.values<br># 评估需要重新评估的个体<br>invalid_ind = [ind for ind in offspring if not ind.fitness.valid]<br>fitnesses = toolbox.map(toolbox.evaluate, invalid_ind)<br>for ind, fit in zip(invalid_ind, fitnesses):<br>ind.fitness.values = fit<br># 更新种群<br>population = toolbox.select(population + offspring, self.population_size)<br>if generation % 10 == 0:<br># 收集当前种群的所有适应度值<br>current_fitnesses = [ind.fitness.values for ind in population if<br>ind.fitness.valid]<br>if current_fitnesses:<br># 计算当前最优目标值（异景程度最大，重复长度最小）<br>max_anomaly = max(f[0] for f in current_fitnesses)<br>min_repetition = min(f[1] for f in current_fitnesses)<br>print(f"第 {generation} 代完成 | 当前最优异景程度: {max_anomaly:.4f} | 当前<br>最优重复长度: {min_repetition:.1f}")<br>else:<br>print(f"第 {generation} 代完成 | 暂无有效适应度数据")<br># 获取帕累托前沿<br>pareto_front = tools.sortNondominated(population, len(population),<br>first_front_only=True)[0]<br># 保存优化结果<br>self.optimal_paths = []<br>self.path_evaluations = []<br>for individual in pareto_front:<br>encoded_individual = list(individual) # 编码格式<br>decoded_path = self._decode_individual(individual) # 解码为节点序列<br>anomaly_score = individual.fitness.values[0]<br>repetition_length = individual.fitness.values[1]<br># 存储解码后的节点序列<br>self.optimal_paths.append(decoded_path)<br>self.path_evaluations.append({<br>'anomaly_score': anomaly_score,<br>'repetition_length': repetition_length,<br>'path_length': len(decoded_path), # 实际节点数量<br>'start_node': decoded_path[0] if decoded_path else None,<br>'end_node': decoded_path[-1] if decoded_path else None,<br>'encoded_individual': encoded_individual # 保留编码格式供参考<br>})<br>print(f"优化完成，获得 {len(self.optimal_paths)} 条帕累托最优路径")<br>return True<br>######################################################################### 缩点<br>######################################################################### 缩点<br>######################################################################### 缩点<br><!-- End of picture text -->

<mark>def simplify_macro_graph(self, distance_threshold=15000, min_cluster_size=2, max_cluster_size=8): """ 简化宏观图：合并距离相近且连通性强的节点 Args: distance_threshold: 节点合并的最大距离阈值 (mm) min_cluster_size: 最小聚类大小 max_cluster_size: 最大聚类大小，防止过度合并 """ if self.macro_graph is None or len(self.macro_graph.nodes) == 0: print("宏观图为空，跳过简化") return print(f"=== 开始图简化 ===") original_nodes = len(self.macro_graph.nodes) original_edges = len(self.macro_graph.edges) # 保护出入口节点，不参与合并 protected_nodes = set(self.entrance_nodes) print(f"保护节点数量: {len(protected_nodes)} (出入口)") # 识别可合并的节点簇 node_clusters = self._identify_node_clusters(distance_threshold, min_cluster_size, max_cluster_size, protected_nodes) if not node_clusters: print("未找到可合并的节点簇") return print(f"识别到 {len(node_clusters)} 个节点簇，准备合并") # 执行节点合并 self._merge_node_clusters(node_clusters) # 清理和重新编号 self._cleanup_graph() new_nodes = len(self.macro_graph.nodes) new_edges = len(self.macro_graph.edges) print(f"图简化完成:") print(f" 节点数: {original_nodes} -> {new_nodes} (减少 {original_nodes-new_nodes})") print(f" 边数: {original_edges} -> {new_edges} (减少 {original_edges-new_edges})") print(f" 简化率: {(original_nodes-new_nodes)/original_nodes*100:.1f}%") def _identify_node_clusters(self, distance_threshold, min_cluster_size, max_cluster_size, protected_nodes): """识别可合并的节点簇""" nodes_to_cluster = [n for n in self.macro_graph.nodes if n not in protected_nodes] if len(nodes_to_cluster) < min_cluster_size: return [] # 构建距离矩阵 node_positions = {} for node in nodes_to_cluster: node_positions[node] = self.macro_graph.nodes[node]['pos'] clusters = [] visited = set() for node in nodes_to_cluster: if node in visited: continue # 使用 BFS 找到连通且距离相近的节点 cluster = self._grow_cluster(node, nodes_to_cluster, node_positions, distance_threshold, max_cluster_size, visited) if len(cluster) >= min_cluster_size: clusters.append(cluster) visited.update(cluster) else: visited.add(node) return clusters</mark> 

125 

<mark>def _grow_cluster(self, seed_node, candidate_nodes, positions, distance_threshold, max_cluster_size, visited): """从种子节点开始生长聚类""" cluster = [seed_node] queue = [seed_node] cluster_set = {seed_node} while queue and len(cluster) < max_cluster_size: current = queue.pop(0) current_pos = positions[current] # 检查所有未访问的候选节点 for candidate in candidate_nodes: if (candidate in visited or candidate in cluster_set or len(cluster) >= max_cluster_size): continue candidate_pos = positions[candidate] distance = np.sqrt((current_pos[0] - candidate_pos[0])**2 + (current_pos[1] - candidate_pos[1])**2) # 距离和连通性检查 if (distance <= distance_threshold and self._check_connectivity(current, candidate, cluster)): cluster.append(candidate) cluster_set.add(candidate) queue.append(candidate) return cluster def _check_connectivity(self, node1, node2, existing_cluster): """检查节点间的连通性""" # 直接相邻 if self.macro_graph.has_edge(node1, node2): return True # 与簇中其他节点的共同邻居数 neighbors1 = set(self.macro_graph.neighbors(node1)) neighbors2 = set(self.macro_graph.neighbors(node2)) # 检查是否有共同邻居 common_neighbors = neighbors1.intersection(neighbors2) if len(common_neighbors) >= 1: return True # 检查是否与簇中已有节点连通 for existing_node in existing_cluster: if self.macro_graph.has_edge(node2, existing_node): return True return False def _merge_node_clusters(self, clusters): """合并节点簇""" node_mapping = {} # 旧节点 -> 新节点的映射 # 获取当前最大节点 ID，确保新 ID 不冲突 max_node_id = max(self.macro_graph.nodes) if self.macro_graph.nodes else 0 for cluster_idx, cluster in enumerate(clusters): if len(cluster) < 2: continue # 创建新的合并节点 ID - 修改为整数类型 new_node_id = max_node_id + cluster_idx + 1 # 使用整数 ID # 计算合并后的节点属性 merged_attributes = self._merge_node_attributes(cluster) # 添加新节点 self.macro_graph.add_node(new_node_id, **merged_attributes) # 记录映射关系 for old_node in cluster: node_mapping[old_node] = new_node_id # 收集所有需要重新连接的边 edges_to_add = self._collect_merged_edges(cluster, new_node_id)</mark> 

126 

<mark># 添加新边 for (u, v), edge_attrs in edges_to_add.items(): if not self.macro_graph.has_edge(u, v): self.macro_graph.add_edge(u, v, **edge_attrs) # 移除旧节点 for old_node in cluster: if old_node in self.macro_graph: self.macro_graph.remove_node(old_node) # 更新出入口节点列表（如果有节点被映射） self.entrance_nodes = [node_mapping.get(node, node) for node in self.entrance_nodes] def _merge_node_attributes(self, cluster): """合并节点属性""" if not cluster: return {} # 收集所有节点的属性 all_attrs = [] total_degree = 0 for node in cluster: attrs = self.macro_graph.nodes[node].copy() all_attrs.append(attrs) total_degree += attrs.get('degree', 1) # 计算位置的加权平均（按度数加权） total_weight = 0 weighted_x = 0 weighted_y = 0 for attrs in all_attrs: weight = attrs.get('degree', 1) pos = attrs['pos'] weighted_x += pos[0] * weight weighted_y += pos[1] * weight total_weight += weight merged_pos = (weighted_x / total_weight, weighted_y / total_weight) # 合并异常程度（取最大值，表示该区域的最高异常水平） max_temporal = max(attrs.get('temporal_anomaly', 0) for attrs in all_attrs) max_spatial = max(attrs.get('spatial_anomaly', 0) for attrs in all_attrs) # 合并其他属性 merged_attrs = { 'pos': merged_pos, 'degree': 0, # 将在重新连接后计算 'temporal_anomaly': max_temporal, 'spatial_anomaly': max_spatial, 'original_nodes': cluster, # 记录原始节点 'merge_reason': 'proximity_and_connectivity', 'is_merged': True # 标记为合并节点 } return merged_attrs def _collect_merged_edges(self, cluster, new_node_id): """收集合并后需要的边""" edges_to_add = {} cluster_set = set(cluster) for node in cluster: for neighbor in self.macro_graph.neighbors(node): if neighbor in cluster_set: continue # 跳过簇内边 # 确定新边的端点 if neighbor < new_node_id: edge_key = (neighbor, new_node_id) else: edge_key = (new_node_id, neighbor) # 合并边属性</mark> 

127 

<mark>old_edge_data = self.macro_graph.edges[node, neighbor] if edge_key in edges_to_add: # 合并多条边的属性 edges_to_add[edge_key] = self._merge_edge_attributes( edges_to_add[edge_key], old_edge_data, new_node_id, neighbor) else: # 重新计算长度，保留其他属性 new_length = self._calculate_edge_length(new_node_id, neighbor) merged_edge_attrs = old_edge_data.copy() merged_edge_attrs['length'] = new_length # 确保 turning_points 属性存在 if 'turning_points' not in merged_edge_attrs: merged_edge_attrs['turning_points'] = 0 edges_to_add[edge_key] = merged_edge_attrs return edges_to_add def _merge_edge_attributes(self, existing_attrs, new_attrs, node1, node2): """合并边属性""" # 重新计算长度 new_length = self._calculate_edge_length(node1, node2) # 合并转折点数（取平均值） existing_turning_points = existing_attrs.get('turning_points', 0) new_turning_points = new_attrs.get('turning_points', 0) merged_turning_points = (existing_turning_points + new_turning_points) / 2 # 异常程度求和（表示该连接的总异常水平） merged_attrs = { 'length': new_length, 'turning_points': merged_turning_points, # 新增 'temporal_anomaly_sum': existing_attrs.get('temporal_anomaly_sum', 0) + new_attrs.get('temporal_anomaly_sum', 0), 'spatial_anomaly_sum': existing_attrs.get('spatial_anomaly_sum', 0) + new_attrs.get('spatial_anomaly_sum', 0), 'merged_edges': existing_attrs.get('merged_edges', 1) + 1 } return merged_attrs def _calculate_edge_length(self, node1, node2): """重新计算边长度""" pos1 = self.macro_graph.nodes[node1]['pos'] pos2 = self.macro_graph.nodes[node2]['pos'] return np.sqrt((pos1[0] - pos2[0])**2 + (pos1[1] - pos2[1])**2) def _cleanup_graph(self): """清理图结构并重新计算度数""" # 重新计算所有节点的度数 for node in self.macro_graph.nodes: self.macro_graph.nodes[node]['degree'] = self.macro_graph.degree[node] # 只保留仍存在于图中的原有出入口节点，不添加新的 original_count = len(self.entrance_nodes) self.entrance_nodes = [node for node in self.entrance_nodes if node in self.macro_graph] print(f"出入口节点: {original_count} -> {len(self.entrance_nodes)}") def main(file_name): """主函数""" # 创建多目标路径规划器 planner = MultiObjectivePathPlanner() # 略 if __name__ == "__main__": # 使用示例 ["寄畅园", "留园", "耦园", "绮园", "秋霞圃", "沈园", "怡园", "豫园", "瞻园", "拙 政园"] file_names = ["近园"] for file_name in file_names: main(file_name)</mark>

'''<br>视景分析<br>获取微观图（路网络）中每个节点的视景<br>'''<br># 导入必要的模块<br>from load_and_visualize import NetworkVisualizer, NetworkLoader<br>import numpy as np<br>import pickle<br>import json<br>from shapely.geometry import Point, LineString<br>from rtree import index<br>import matplotlib.pyplot as plt<br>from sklearn.metrics.pairwise import cosine_similarity<br>from scipy.spatial.distance import hamming<br>from concurrent.futures import ProcessPoolExecutor, as_completed<br>from multiprocessing import cpu_count<br># 设置中文字体<br>plt.rcParams['font.family'] = ['simhei']<br>plt.rcParams['axes.unicode_minus'] = False<br>def compute_point_visibility_matrix(args):<br>"""<br>独立的进程计算函数<br>Args:<br>args: (point_id, point, analyzer_params, segments_info_data)<br>Returns:<br>tuple: (point_id, result_dict)<br>"""<br>point_id, point, analyzer_params, segments_info_data = args<br># 在进程内重建分析器状态<br>analyzer = VisibilityAnalyzer(<br>angle_step=analyzer_params['angle_step'],<br>max_distance=analyzer_params['max_distance']<br>)<br># 重建 segments_info 和空间索引<br>analyzer.segments_info = segments_info_data<br># 重建空间索引<br>from rtree import index<br>spatial_idx = index.Index()<br>for i, segment_info in enumerate(analyzer.segments_info):<br>spatial_idx.insert(i, segment_info['geometry'].bounds)<br>analyzer.spatial_index = spatial_idx<br># 计算视景矩阵<br>try:<br>visibility_matrix = analyzer.compute_visibility_matrix(point)<br>return point_id, {<br>'point': point,<br>'matrix': visibility_matrix<br>}<br>except Exception as e:<br>print(f"进程计算节点 {point_id} 时出错: {e}")<br>return point_id, None<br>class VisibilityAnalyzer:<br>"""视景分析器"""<br><!-- End of picture text -->

129 



<!-- Start of picture text -->
def __init__(self, angle_step=3, max_distance=100000): # 距离单位为 mm<br>"""<br>初始化视景分析器<br>Args:<br>angle_step: 射线角度间隔（度）<br>max_distance: 最大视景距离（mm）<br>"""<br>self.angle_step = angle_step<br>self.max_distance = max_distance<br>self.num_rays = 360 // angle_step<br>self.distance_step = 1000 # 每 1 米采样一次<br>self.num_distance_points = max_distance // self.distance_step<br># 景观元素编号映射<br>self.element_mapping = {<br>'被遮挡': 0,<br>'半开放建筑': 1,<br>'实体建筑': 2,<br>'道路': 3,<br>'假山': 4,<br>'水体': 5,<br>'植物': 6<br>}<br># 遮挡性元素（只有实体建筑会完全遮挡视线）<br>self.occlusion_elements = {'实体建筑'}<br>print(f"初始化视景分析器: {self.num_rays}条射线, 最大距离{max_distance/1000:.1f}m")<br>def _prepare_process_args(self):<br>"""准备进程计算所需的参数"""<br>analyzer_params = {<br>'angle_step': self.angle_step,<br>'max_distance': self.max_distance<br>}<br># 准备 segments_info 数据（需要可序列化）<br>segments_info_data = []<br>for segment_info in self.segments_info:<br>segments_info_data.append({<br>'id': segment_info['id'],<br>'element_type': segment_info['element_type'],<br>'geometry': segment_info['geometry'] # Shapely 对象可以被 pickle 序列化<br>})<br>return analyzer_params, segments_info_data<br>def build_spatial_index(self, geometry_data):<br>"""构建空间索引"""<br>print("构建空间索引...")<br># 创建 R-tree 空间索引<br>spatial_idx = index.Index()<br># 存储线段信息<br>self.segments_info = []<br>segment_id = 0<br>for element_type, data in geometry_data.items():<br>if element_type == "植物":<br># 植物处理为圆形边界<br>for plant in data:<br>center = plant['center']<br>radius = plant['radius']<br># 简化为正八边形近似圆<br>angles = np.linspace(0, 2*np.pi, 9)[:-1] # 8 个顶点<br>vertices = [(center[0] + radius * np.cos(a),<br>center[1] + radius * np.sin(a)) for a in angles]<br><!-- End of picture text -->

130 

<mark># 创建边界线段 for i in range(len(vertices)): start = vertices[i] end = vertices[(i + 1) % len(vertices)] line = LineString([start, end]) # 添加到索引 spatial_idx.insert(segment_id, line.bounds) self.segments_info.append({ 'id': segment_id, 'element_type': element_type, 'geometry': line }) segment_id += 1 else: # 处理线段数据 for segment in data.get('segments', []): if len(segment) > 1: for i in range(len(segment) - 1): start = segment[i] end = segment[i + 1] line = LineString([start, end]) # 添加到索引 spatial_idx.insert(segment_id, line.bounds) self.segments_info.append({ 'id': segment_id, 'element_type': element_type, 'geometry': line }) segment_id += 1 self.spatial_index = spatial_idx print(f"空间索引构建完成: {len(self.segments_info)} 个线段") return spatial_idx def cast_ray(self, observer_point, angle_deg): """ 发射射线并检测交点，根据交点进行区域填充 Args: observer_point: 观察点坐标 (x, y) angle_deg: 射线角度（度） Returns: list: 射线上的景观序列 """ angle_rad = np.radians(angle_deg) # 射线终点（稍微延长以确保能找到边界交点） extend_distance = self.max_distance * 1.2 # 延长 20% end_x = observer_point[0] + extend_distance * np.cos(angle_rad) end_y = observer_point[1] + extend_distance * np.sin(angle_rad) ray = LineString([observer_point, (end_x, end_y)]) # 找到所有交点 intersections = [] for seg_id in self.spatial_index.intersection(ray.bounds): segment_info = self.segments_info[seg_id] # 跳过路网络，不对景观序列产生影响 if segment_info['element_type'] == '路网络': continue intersection = ray.intersection(segment_info['geometry']) if not intersection.is_empty: # 处理交点坐标</mark> 

131 



<!-- Start of picture text -->
if hasattr(intersection, 'coords'): # Point<br>point_coords = list(intersection.coords)[0]<br>elif hasattr(intersection, 'geoms'): # MultiPoint - 取最近的点<br>points = [list(geom.coords)[0] for geom in intersection.geoms if<br>hasattr(geom, 'coords')]<br>if points:<br># 选择最近的交点<br>distances = [np.sqrt((p[0] - observer_point[0])**2 + (p[1] -<br>observer_point[1])**2) for p in points]<br>point_coords = points[np.argmin(distances)]<br>else:<br>continue<br>else:<br>continue<br>distance = np.sqrt((point_coords[0] - observer_point[0])**2 +<br>(point_coords[1] - observer_point[1])**2)<br># 只考虑正方向的交点<br>if distance > 1: # 忽略太近的点（避免数值误差）<br>intersections.append({<br>'distance': distance,<br>'element_type': segment_info['element_type'],<br>'point': point_coords<br>})<br># 按距离排序交点<br>intersections.sort(key=lambda x: x['distance'])<br># 构建景观序列<br>landscape_sequence = []<br>current_distance = 0<br>intersection_idx = 0<br>current_element = None # 当前区域的景观类型<br>is_occluded = False # 是否被遮挡<br>while current_distance < self.max_distance:<br># 检查是否到达新的交点<br>while (intersection_idx < len(intersections) and<br>intersections[intersection_idx]['distance'] <= current_distance):<br>element_type = intersections[intersection_idx]['element_type']<br>current_element = element_type<br>intersection_idx += 1<br># 如果遇到遮挡性元素，在处理完当前点后标记遮挡<br>if element_type in self.occlusion_elements:<br>is_occluded = True<br># 确定当前距离点的景观类型<br>if current_element is not None:<br># 在某个景观区域内（包括实体建筑）<br>landscape_element = current_element<br>else:<br># 还没有遇到任何边界，需要特殊处理<br>future_intersection = None<br>for i in range(intersection_idx, len(intersections)):<br>if intersections[i]['distance'] <= self.max_distance * 1.2:<br>future_intersection = intersections[i]<br>break<br>if future_intersection:<br>landscape_element = future_intersection['element_type']<br>else:<br>landscape_element = '被遮挡'<br># 记录当前距离的景观元素<br>landscape_sequence.append(self.element_mapping.get(landscape_element, 0))<br>current_distance += self.distance_step<br># 如果已经被遮挡，从下一个点开始填充"被遮挡"<br><!-- End of picture text -->

132 



<!-- Start of picture text -->
if is_occluded:<br>remaining_points = int((self.max_distance - current_distance) //<br>self.distance_step)<br>landscape_sequence.extend([self.element_mapping['被遮挡']] *<br>remaining_points)<br>break<br># 确保序列长度正确<br>while len(landscape_sequence) < self.num_distance_points:<br>landscape_sequence.append(landscape_sequence[-1] if landscape_sequence else 0)<br>return landscape_sequence[:self.num_distance_points]<br>def compute_visibility_matrix(self, observer_point):<br>"""<br>计算观察点的视景矩阵<br>Args:<br>observer_point: 观察点坐标 (x, y)<br>Returns:<br>np.array: 视景矩阵 (num_rays, num_distance_points)<br>"""<br>visibility_matrix = []<br>for i in range(self.num_rays):<br>angle = i * self.angle_step<br>landscape_sequence = self.cast_ray(observer_point, angle)<br># 确保序列长度一致<br>if len(landscape_sequence) < self.num_distance_points:<br># 用最后一个元素填充<br>last_element = landscape_sequence[-1] if landscape_sequence else 0<br>landscape_sequence.extend([last_element] *<br>(self.num_distance_points - len(landscape_sequence)))<br>elif len(landscape_sequence) > self.num_distance_points:<br>landscape_sequence = landscape_sequence[:self.num_distance_points]<br>visibility_matrix.append(landscape_sequence)<br>return np.array(visibility_matrix)<br>def _compute_single_point_matrix(self, point_info):<br>"""<br>计算单个点的视景矩阵（线程安全版本）<br>Args:<br>point_info: (point_id, point_coordinates)<br>Returns:<br>tuple: (point_id, result_dict)<br>"""<br>point_id, point = point_info<br>visibility_matrix = self.compute_visibility_matrix(point)<br>return point_id, {<br>'point': point,<br>'matrix': visibility_matrix<br>}<br>def compute_all_visibility_matrices(self, network_points):<br>"""<br>计算所有路网络节点的视景矩阵<br>Args:<br>network_points: 路网络节点列表 [(x, y), ...]<br>Returns:<br>dict: {point_id: visibility_matrix}<br>"""<br><!-- End of picture text -->

<!-- Start of picture text -->
print(f"计算 {len(network_points)} 个节点的视景矩阵...")<br>visibility_matrices = {}<br>for i, point in enumerate(network_points):<br>if i % 10 == 0:<br>print(f"进度: {i+1}/{len(network_points)}")<br>visibility_matrix = self.compute_visibility_matrix(point)<br>visibility_matrices[i] = {<br>'point': point,<br>'matrix': visibility_matrix<br>}<br>print("视景矩阵计算完成")<br>return visibility_matrices<br>def compute_all_visibility_matrices_bingfa(self, network_points, max_workers=None):<br>"""<br>计算所有路网络节点的视景矩阵（进程池版本）<br>Args:<br>network_points: 路网络节点列表 [(x, y), ...]<br>max_workers: 最大工作进程数，默认为 None（自动选择）<br>Returns:<br>dict: {point_id: visibility_matrix}<br>"""<br>print(f"计算 {len(network_points)} 个节点的视景矩阵...")<br># 如果未指定进程数，使用 CPU 核心数<br>if max_workers is None:<br>max_workers = min(cpu_count(), len(network_points))<br>print(f"使用 {max_workers} 个并发进程")<br># 准备进程计算参数<br>analyzer_params, segments_info_data = self._prepare_process_args()<br># 准备任务参数<br>task_args = []<br>for i, point in enumerate(network_points):<br>task_args.append((i, point, analyzer_params, segments_info_data))<br>visibility_matrices = {}<br>completed_count = 0<br># 使用进程池并发处理<br>with ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=max_workers) as executor:<br># 提交所有任务<br>future_to_point = {<br>executor.submit(compute_point_visibility_matrix, args): args[0]<br>for args in task_args<br>}<br># 处理完成的任务<br>for future in as_completed(future_to_point):<br>point_id = future_to_point[future]<br>try:<br>result_point_id, result = future.result()<br>if result is not None:<br>visibility_matrices[result_point_id] = result<br>completed_count += 1<br># 显示进度<br>if completed_count % 5 == 0 or completed_count == len(network_points):<br>print(f"进度: {completed_count}/{len(network_points)}")<br>except Exception as e:<br><!-- End of picture text -->

134 

<mark>print(f"处理节点 {point_id} 的结果时出错: {e}") # 显式关闭执行器 executor.shutdown(wait=True) print("视景矩阵计算完成") return visibility_matrices def compute_landscape_transition_probabilities(self, visibility_matrices, network_connections): """ 计算景观类别转移概率矩阵（马尔可夫） Args: visibility_matrices: 视景矩阵字典 network_connections: 网络连接关系 [(point_id1, point_id2), ...] Returns: np.array: 转移概率矩阵 (7, 7) """ print("计算景观转移概率...") # 统计转移次数 transition_counts = np.zeros((7, 7)) # 7 种景观类型 for point_id1, point_id2 in network_connections: if point_id1 in visibility_matrices and point_id2 in visibility_matrices: matrix1 = visibility_matrices[point_id1]['matrix'] matrix2 = visibility_matrices[point_id2]['matrix'] # 统计每个位置的景观转移 for i in range(matrix1.shape[0]): for j in range(matrix1.shape[1]): from_element = matrix1[i, j] to_element = matrix2[i, j] transition_counts[from_element, to_element] += 1 # 转换为概率 row_sums = transition_counts.sum(axis=1) transition_probs = np.divide(transition_counts, row_sums.reshape(-1, 1), out=np.zeros_like(transition_counts), where=row_sums.reshape(-1, 1) != 0) return transition_probs def compute_anomaly_score(self, point_id, visibility_matrices, network_connections, transition_probs): """ 计算单点异景程度：该点与直接相连接点的视景矩阵差异度量 """ if point_id not in visibility_matrices: return {'temporal_anomaly': 0, 'spatial_anomaly': 0} current_matrix = visibility_matrices[point_id]['matrix'] # 找到所有直接相邻的点 neighbor_ids = [] for conn in network_connections: if conn[0] == point_id and conn[1] in visibility_matrices: neighbor_ids.append(conn[1]) elif conn[1] == point_id and conn[0] in visibility_matrices: neighbor_ids.append(conn[0]) if not neighbor_ids: return {'temporal_anomaly': 0, 'spatial_anomaly': 0} temporal_anomalies = [] spatial_anomalies = [] for neighbor_id in neighbor_ids: neighbor_matrix = visibility_matrices[neighbor_id]['matrix'] # 1. 时间维度异景度：马尔可夫转移的意外性 # 把从当前点移动到邻居点看作时间序列中的状态转移 temporal_anomaly = 0</mark> 

135 

<mark>valid_transitions = 0 # 对每个角度-距离位置，计算景观类别转移的意外性 for i in range(current_matrix.shape[0]): # 角度 for j in range(current_matrix.shape[1]): # 距离 from_element = current_matrix[i, j] # 当前点的景观 to_element = neighbor_matrix[i, j] # 邻居点的景观 # 查询转移概率 prob = transition_probs[from_element, to_element] if prob > 0: # 低概率转移 = 高异景程度（信息熵） temporal_anomaly += -np.log(prob) valid_transitions += 1 elif from_element != to_element: # 如果转移概率为 0 但景观发生变化，视为极高异景 temporal_anomaly += 10 # 惩罚项 valid_transitions += 1 if valid_transitions > 0: temporal_anomaly /= valid_transitions temporal_anomalies.append(temporal_anomaly) # 2. 空间维度异景度：视景矩阵结构相似性 # 计算两个视景矩阵的结构差异 # 汉明距离（适合离散分类数据） current_flat = current_matrix.flatten() neighbor_flat = neighbor_matrix.flatten() hamming_dist = np.mean(current_flat != neighbor_flat) spatial_anomalies.append(hamming_dist) # 计算平均异景度 temporal_anomaly = np.mean(temporal_anomalies) if temporal_anomalies else 0 spatial_anomaly = np.mean(spatial_anomalies) if spatial_anomalies else 0 return { 'temporal_anomaly': float(temporal_anomaly), 'spatial_anomaly': float(spatial_anomaly), } def save_results(self, visibility_matrices, anomaly_scores, transition_probs, filename_prefix): """保存计算结果""" print(f"保存结果到 {filename_prefix}...") # 保存视景矩阵 with open(f"{filename_prefix}_visibility_matrices.pkl", 'wb') as f: pickle.dump(visibility_matrices, f) # 保存异景程度 with open(f"{filename_prefix}_anomaly_scores.json", 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f: json.dump(anomaly_scores, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2) # 保存转移概率矩阵 np.save(f"{filename_prefix}_transition_probs.npy", transition_probs) # 保存分析参数 params = { 'angle_step': self.angle_step, 'max_distance': self.max_distance, 'distance_step': self.distance_step, 'num_rays': self.num_rays, 'num_distance_points': self.num_distance_points, 'element_mapping': self.element_mapping } with open(f"{filename_prefix}_params.json", 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f: json.dump(params, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2) print("结果保存完成") def extract_network_connections(geometry_data): """从几何数据中提取网络连接关系""" connections =</mark> <u><mark>[]</mark></u>

<mark>if "路网络" not in geometry_data: return connections network_data = geometry_data["路网络"] # 从 segments 中提取连接关系 point_to_id = {} point_id = 0 # 为所有点分配 ID for segment in network_data.get('segments', []): for point in segment: point_key = (round(point[0], 1), round(point[1], 1)) # 四舍五入避免浮点精度问题 if point_key not in point_to_id: point_to_id[point_key] = point_id point_id += 1 # 提取连接关系 for segment in network_data.get('segments', []): for i in range(len(segment) - 1): point1 = (round(segment[i][0], 1), round(segment[i][1], 1)) point2 = (round(segment[i+1][0], 1), round(segment[i+1][1], 1)) id1 = point_to_id[point1] id2 = point_to_id[point2] connections.append((id1, id2)) return connections, list(point_to_id.keys()) def main(file_name): """主函数""" # 加载数据 loader = NetworkLoader() original_excel = f"shujv/4-{file_name}数据坐标.xlsx" network_excel = f"{file_name}-路网络.xlsx" print("加载数据...") geometry_data = loader.load_original_data(original_excel) network_data = loader.load_network_data(network_excel) if geometry_data is None or network_data is None: print("数据加载失败") return geometry_data["路网络"] = network_data print("构建路网络...") # 提取网络连接和节点 # connections: 列表，其中为元组（A, B）; network_points_keys: 列表，点 ID connections, network_points_keys = extract_network_connections(geometry_data) network_points = [point for point in network_points_keys] print(f"网络节点数: {len(network_points)}") print(f"网络连接数: {len(connections)}") print(f"=== 视景分析: {file_name} ===") # 创建视景分析器 analyzer = VisibilityAnalyzer(angle_step=3, max_distance=100000) # 100 米 # 构建空间索引 analyzer.build_spatial_index(geometry_data) # 只处理前 5 个点进行测试 ''' test_points = network_points[:5] print(f"测试模式：只处理前 {len(test_points)} 个点") visibility_matrices = analyzer.compute_all_visibility_matrices_bingfa(test_points, max_workers=5) # 相应地，connections 也需要过滤，只保留涉及这些点的连接 test_connections = [] for conn in connections: if conn[0] < len(test_points) and conn[1] < len(test_points): test_connections.append(conn) connections = test_connections '''</mark>

# 计算视景矩阵（字典 {point_id: visibility_matrix} ）<br>#visibility_matrices = analyzer.compute_all_visibility_matrices(network_points)<br># 并发版本<br>visibility_matrices = analyzer.compute_all_visibility_matrices_bingfa(network_points,<br>max_workers=8)<br># 计算转移概率<br>transition_probs = analyzer.compute_landscape_transition_probabilities(<br>visibility_matrices, connections)<br># 逐个点计算异景程度（字典，键为点 ID，值为异景分数）<br>anomaly_scores = {}<br>for point_id in visibility_matrices.keys():<br>anomaly_scores[point_id] = analyzer.compute_anomaly_score(<br>point_id, visibility_matrices, connections, transition_probs)<br># 保存结果<br>analyzer.save_results(visibility_matrices, anomaly_scores, transition_probs,<br>f"{file_name}_visibility_analysis")<br># 可视化示例（第一个节点）<br>if visibility_matrices:<br>first_point_id = list(visibility_matrices.keys())[0]<br>analyzer.visualize_visibility_matrix(<br>first_point_id, visibility_matrices,<br>f"{file_name}_visibility_matrix_example.png")<br>return visibility_matrices, anomaly_scores, transition_probs<br>if __name__ == "__main__":<br># 使用示例拙政园<br>file_names = ["近园"]<br>for file_name in file_names:<br>visibility_matrices, anomaly_scores, transition_probs = main(file_name)<br>voronoi_path_builder.py<br>'''<br>Voronoi 路径网络<br>构建、筛选边与子图、保存<br>'''<br>import pandas as pd<br>import numpy as np<br>import matplotlib.pyplot as plt<br>from matplotlib.patches import Polygon, Circle<br>from shapely.geometry import Point, LineString, Polygon as ShapelyPolygon<br>from rtree import index<br>import networkx as nx<br>from scipy.spatial import Voronoi<br>import copy<br>class SimpleVoronoiPathBuilder:<br>"""简化的 Voronoi 路径网络构建器"""<br>def __init__(self, geometry_data):<br>"""<br>初始化路径构建器<br>Args:<br>geometry_data: 几何数据字典<br>"""<br>self.geometry_data = geometry_data<br>self.obstacle_types = ["半开放建筑", "实体建筑", "道路", "障碍物连接"] # 影响通行的障碍<br>物线段<br>self.boundary_points = []<br>self.obstacle_segments = [] # 已有<br>self.obstacle_lines = [] # 新增：预处理的障碍物线段<br><!-- End of picture text -->

138 

<mark>self.voronoi = None self.voronoi_edges = [] self.path_network = None # 路网络的完整路径 self.pruned_network = None # 路网络的剪枝路径 def preprocess_obstacle_lines(self): """预处理障碍物线段为 shapely LineString 对象，用于相交检测""" print("正在预处理障碍物线段...") self.obstacle_lines = [] for segment in self.obstacle_segments: if len(segment) >= 2: # 将每个 segment 转换为多条 LineString for i in range(len(segment) - 1): try: start_point = segment[i] end_point = segment[i + 1] if (start_point != end_point and not any(np.isnan([start_point[0], start_point[1], end_point[0], end_point[1]])) and not any(np.isinf([start_point[0], start_point[1], end_point[0], end_point[1]]))): line = LineString([start_point, end_point]) if line.length > 1e-6: # 确保线段有实际长度 self.obstacle_lines.append(line) except Exception as e: continue print(f"预处理完成，共 {len(self.obstacle_lines)} 条有效障碍物线段") return self.obstacle_lines def collect_boundary_points(self): """收集障碍物边界点，并添加外边界""" print("正在收集边界点...") all_points = [] all_segments = [] for obstacle_type in self.obstacle_types: if obstacle_type in self.geometry_data: data = self.geometry_data[obstacle_type] # 收集 segments 中的点 for segment in data.get('segments', []): all_points.extend(segment) all_segments.append(segment) # 收集 all_points all_points.extend(data.get('all_points', [])) # 去重 unique_points = [] for point in all_points: if point not in unique_points: unique_points.append(point) # 添加外边界点来约束 Voronoi 图 if unique_points: x_coords = [p[0] for p in unique_points] y_coords = [p[1] for p in unique_points] # 计算边界范围，并扩展一些边距 margin = max(max(x_coords) - min(x_coords), max(y_coords) - min(y_coords)) * 0.1 x_min, x_max = min(x_coords) - margin, max(x_coords) + margin y_min, y_max = min(y_coords) - margin, max(y_coords) + margin # 添加边界点（四个角和边上的一些点） boundary_density = 10 # 每条边上的点数 # 添加四个角 boundary_points = [</mark> <u><mark>(x_min, y_min), (x_max, y_min),</mark></u> 

139 

<mark>(x_max, y_max), (x_min, y_max) ] # 添加边上的点 for i in range(1, boundary_density): t = i / boundary_density # 下边 boundary_points.append((x_min + t * (x_max - x_min), y_min)) # 上边 boundary_points.append((x_min + t * (x_max - x_min), y_max)) # 左边 boundary_points.append((x_min, y_min + t * (y_max - y_min))) # 右边 boundary_points.append((x_max, y_min + t * (y_max - y_min))) unique_points.extend(boundary_points) self.boundary_points = unique_points self.obstacle_segments = all_segments print(f"收集到 {len(self.boundary_points)} 个边界点（包含外边界）") print(f"收集到 {len(self.obstacle_segments)} 个障碍物线段") return self.boundary_points def generate_voronoi_diagram(self): """生成 Voronoi 图""" if len(self.boundary_points) < 4: print(f"边界点太少({len(self.boundary_points)}个)，无法生成 Voronoi 图") return None print("正在生成 Voronoi 图...") try: # 转换为 numpy 数组 points_array = np.array(self.boundary_points) # 生成 Voronoi 图 self.voronoi = Voronoi(points_array) print(f"生成了 {len(self.voronoi.vertices)} 个 Voronoi 顶点") print(f"生成了 {len(self.voronoi.ridge_vertices)} 条 Voronoi 边") return self.voronoi except Exception as e: print(f"生成 Voronoi 图失败: {e}") return None def extract_voronoi_edges(self): """提取 Voronoi 边作为路径""" if self.voronoi is None: print("请先生成 Voronoi 图") return [] print("正在提取 Voronoi 边...") edges = [] for ridge_vertices in self.voronoi.ridge_vertices: # 跳过无限边（包含-1 的边） if -1 in ridge_vertices: continue # 获取边的两个端点 vertex1 = self.voronoi.vertices[ridge_vertices[0]] vertex2 = self.voronoi.vertices[ridge_vertices[1]] # 简单的有效性检查 if (not np.isnan(vertex1).any() and not np.isnan(vertex2).any() and not np.isinf(vertex1).any() and not np.isinf(vertex2).any()): edges.append((tuple(vertex1), tuple(vertex2))) self.voronoi_edges = edges print(f"提取到 {len(edges)} 条有效的 Voronoi 边") return edges def create_spatial_index(self): """为障碍物线段创建空间索引以加速相交检测（需要安装 rtree 库）""" # 创建空间索引</mark> 

140 

<mark>idx = index.Index() for i, line in enumerate(self.obstacle_lines): # 获取线段的边界框 bounds = line.bounds # (minx, miny, maxx, maxy) idx.insert(i, bounds) self.spatial_index = idx print(f"创建空间索引完成，包含 {len(self.obstacle_lines)} 条线段") return True def filter_voronoi_edges_with_spatial_index(self, max_edge_length=None, intersection_tolerance=1e-6): """使用空间索引优化的边过滤方法""" if not self.voronoi_edges: return [] print("正在过滤 Voronoi 边（使用空间索引）...")</mark> 



<mark>if max_edge_length is None:</mark> 

<mark>all_x = [p[0] for p in self.boundary_points] all_y = [p[1] for p in self.boundary_points] diagonal = np.sqrt((max(all_x) - min(all_x))**2 + (max(all_y) - min(all_y))**2) max_edge_length = diagonal * 0.05 # 预处理和创建索引 if not self.obstacle_lines: self.preprocess_obstacle_lines() use_spatial_index = self.create_spatial_index() filtered_edges = [] length_filtered = 0 intersection_filtered = 0 for i, edge in enumerate(self.voronoi_edges): start, end = edge # 基本检查 if (any(np.isnan([start[0], start[1], end[0], end[1]])) or any(np.isinf([start[0], start[1], end[0], end[1]]))): continue # 长度检查 edge_length = np.sqrt((start[0] - end[0])**2 + (start[1] - end[1])**2) if edge_length > max_edge_length: length_filtered += 1 continue # 相交检查 intersects_obstacle = False try: voronoi_line = LineString([start, end]) if voronoi_line.length < intersection_tolerance: continue # 使用空间索引或普通遍历 if use_spatial_index and self.spatial_index: # 获取可能相交的线段索引 bounds = voronoi_line.bounds possible_matches = list(self.spatial_index.intersection(bounds)) candidate_lines = [self.obstacle_lines[j] for j in possible_matches] else: candidate_lines = self.obstacle_lines # 检查相交 for obstacle_line in candidate_lines: if voronoi_line.intersects(obstacle_line): intersection = voronoi_line.intersection(obstacle_line) if hasattr(intersection, 'geom_type'): if intersection.geom_type in ['LineString', 'MultiLineString']: intersects_obstacle = True break elif intersection.geom_type == 'Point':</mark>

<!-- Start of picture text -->
point_coords = (intersection.x, intersection.y)<br>start_dist = np.sqrt((point_coords[0] - start[0])**2 +<br>(point_coords[1] - start[1])**2)<br>end_dist = np.sqrt((point_coords[0] - end[0])**2 +<br>(point_coords[1] - end[1])**2)<br>if (start_dist > intersection_tolerance and<br>end_dist > intersection_tolerance):<br>intersects_obstacle = True<br>break<br>if intersects_obstacle:<br>intersection_filtered += 1<br>else:<br>filtered_edges.append(edge)<br>except Exception as e:<br>intersection_filtered += 1<br>continue<br>if (i + 1) % 1000 == 0:<br>print(f"已处理 {i + 1}/{len(self.voronoi_edges)} 条边...")<br>self.voronoi_edges = filtered_edges<br>return filtered_edges<br>def analyze_connected_components(self):<br>"""分析连通分量并只保留第 1 大的子图"""<br>if not self.voronoi_edges:<br>print("没有可用的边来分析连通分量")<br>return None<br>print("正在分析连通分量...")<br># 构建临时网络图用于连通性分析<br>temp_G = nx.Graph()<br>for edge in self.voronoi_edges:<br>start_node = edge[0]<br>end_node = edge[1]<br>weight = np.sqrt((start_node[0] - end_node[0])**2 + (start_node[1] -<br>end_node[1])**2)<br>temp_G.add_edge(start_node, end_node, weight=weight)<br># 获取所有连通分量<br>connected_components = list(nx.connected_components(temp_G))<br>print(f"发现 {len(connected_components)} 个连通分量")<br># 计算每个连通分量的统计信息<br>component_stats = []<br>for i, component in enumerate(connected_components):<br>subgraph = temp_G.subgraph(component)<br>num_nodes = subgraph.number_of_nodes()<br>num_edges = subgraph.number_of_edges()<br># 计算综合得分（边数+点数）<br>score = num_edges + num_nodes<br>component_stats.append({<br>'index': i,<br>'nodes': num_nodes,<br>'edges': num_edges,<br>'score': score,<br>'component': component,<br>'subgraph': subgraph<br>})<br># 按综合得分排序，只保留第 1 大<br>component_stats.sort(key=lambda x: x['score'], reverse=True)<br>largest_component = component_stats[0] if component_stats else None<br>if largest_component:<br>print(f"保留最大连通分量: {largest_component['nodes']}个节点,<br>{largest_component['edges']}条边 (得分: {largest_component['score']})")<br># 提取最大子图的边<br><!-- End of picture text -->

142 

<mark>self.filtered_edges_by_component = [] self.component_info = [largest_component] component_edges = [] subgraph = largest_component['subgraph'] for edge in subgraph.edges(): # 查找原始边信息 for original_edge in self.voronoi_edges: if ((original_edge[0] == edge[0] and original_edge[1] == edge[1]) or (original_edge[0] == edge[1] and original_edge[1] == edge[0])): component_edges.append(original_edge) break self.filtered_edges_by_component.append(component_edges) self.all_voronoi_edges = copy.deepcopy(self.voronoi_edges) # 更新 voronoi_edges 为过滤后的边 self.voronoi_edges = component_edges print(f"过滤后保留 {len(self.voronoi_edges)} 条边") else: print("未找到有效的连通分量") self.filtered_edges_by_component = [] self.component_info = [] self.voronoi_edges = [] return self.component_info def build_filtered_network_graph(self): """基于过滤后的连通分量构建网络图""" if not hasattr(self, 'filtered_edges_by_component'): print("请先进行连通性分析") return None print("正在构建过滤后的网络图...") # 创建网络图 G = nx.Graph() # 为每个连通分量分配不同的属性 for comp_idx, component_edges in enumerate(self.filtered_edges_by_component): for edge in component_edges: start_node = edge[0] end_node = edge[1] weight = np.sqrt((start_node[0] - end_node[0])**2 + (start_node[1] - end_node[1])**2) # 添加边，并标记所属的连通分量 G.add_edge(start_node, end_node, weight=weight, component=comp_idx) # 为节点添加连通分量信息 for comp_idx, component_edges in enumerate(self.filtered_edges_by_component): for edge in component_edges: G.nodes[edge[0]]['component'] = comp_idx G.nodes[edge[1]]['component'] = comp_idx self.path_network = G print(f"过滤后的网络图包含 {G.number_of_nodes()} 个节点和 {G.number_of_edges()} 条边") return G def set_start_end_regions(self, baohu_regions=None): """ 设置起点和终点区域 Args: baohu_regions: 端点保护区域，每个区域为 {'center': (x, y), 'radius': r} 或多边形顶 点列表 """ self.baohu_regions = baohu_regions if baohu_regions else [] print(f"设置了 {len(self.baohu_regions)} 个端点保护区域") def identify_protected_nodes(self): """识别在起点终点区域内的受保护节点""" if not self.path_network:</mark> 

143 



<!-- Start of picture text -->
print("请先构建路径网络")<br>return set()<br>self.protected_nodes = set()<br>all_regions = self.baohu_regions<br>for node in self.path_network.nodes():<br>node_point = Point(node[0], node[1])<br>for region in all_regions:<br># 检查是否在圆形区域内<br>if isinstance(region, dict) and 'center' in region and 'radius' in region:<br>center = region['center']<br>radius = region['radius']<br>distance = np.sqrt((node[0] - center[0])**2 + (node[1] - center[1])**2)<br>if distance <= radius:<br>self.protected_nodes.add(node)<br>break<br># 检查是否在多边形区域内<br>elif isinstance(region, list) and len(region) >= 3:<br>from shapely.geometry import Polygon as ShapelyPolygon<br>try:<br>polygon = ShapelyPolygon(region)<br>if polygon.contains(node_point):<br>self.protected_nodes.add(node)<br>break<br>except:<br>continue<br>print(f"识别到 {len(self.protected_nodes)} 个受保护节点")<br>return self.protected_nodes<br>def prune_branches(self, max_iterations=1000):<br>"""<br>修剪分支：迭代移除度为 1 的非保护节点<br>Args:<br>max_iterations: 最大迭代次数，防止无限循环<br>Returns:<br>修剪后的网络图<br>"""<br>if not self.path_network:<br>print("请先构建路径网络")<br>return None<br>if not self.protected_nodes:<br>print("未设置保护节点，将识别起点终点区域内的节点")<br>self.identify_protected_nodes()<br># 创建网络的深拷贝用于修剪<br>self.pruned_network = copy.deepcopy(self.path_network)<br>print("开始修剪分支...")<br>iteration = 0<br>removed_total = 0<br>while iteration < max_iterations:<br># 找到所有度为 1 的非保护节点<br>nodes_to_remove = []<br>for node in self.pruned_network.nodes():<br>if (self.pruned_network.degree(node) == 1 and<br>node not in self.protected_nodes):<br>nodes_to_remove.append(node)<br># 如果没有可移除的节点，结束修剪<br>if not nodes_to_remove:<br>print(f"修剪完成，无更多可移除的分支节点")<br>break<br><!-- End of picture text -->

144 

<mark># 移除节点（自动删除所有连接到该节点的边） for node in nodes_to_remove: self.pruned_network.remove_node(node) removed_total += 1 iteration += 1 print(f"第 {iteration} 轮修剪：移除 {len(nodes_to_remove)} 个节点") if iteration >= max_iterations: print(f"达到最大迭代次数 {max_iterations}，修剪结束") return self.pruned_network def build_network_graph(self): """构建网络图""" if not self.voronoi_edges: print("没有可用的边来构建网络") return None print("正在构建网络图...") # 创建网络图 G = nx.Graph() # 添加边 for edge in self.voronoi_edges: start_node = edge[0] end_node = edge[1] # 计算边的权重（长度） weight = np.sqrt((start_node[0] - end_node[0])**2 + (start_node[1] - end_node[1])**2) G.add_edge(start_node, end_node, weight=weight) self.path_network = G print(f"网络图包含 {G.number_of_nodes()} 个节点和 {G.number_of_edges()} 条边") return G def build_complete_path_network(self, enable_pruning=True): """完整的路径网络构建流程""" print("\n=== 开始构建 Voronoi 路径网络 ===") # 1. 收集边界点 self.collect_boundary_points() # 2. 生成 Voronoi 图 self.generate_voronoi_diagram() if self.voronoi is None: print("Voronoi 图生成失败，无法继续") return None # 3. 提取边 self.extract_voronoi_edges() # 3.5. 预处理障碍物线段 self.preprocess_obstacle_lines() # 4. 过滤边(使用空间索引优化的边过滤方法 self.filter_voronoi_edges_with_spatial_index() # 5. 连通性分析和子图过滤 self.analyze_connected_components() # 6. 构建过滤后的网络图 self.build_filtered_network_graph() # 7. 分支修剪（新增） if enable_pruning and self.path_network: print("\n=== 开始分支修剪 ===") self.identify_protected_nodes() self.prune_branches() print("=== Voronoi 路径网络构建完成 ===\n") return self.pruned_network if enable_pruning else self.path_network def save_pruned_network_to_excel(self, file_name): """ 将修剪后的路径网络 pruned_network 保存到 Excel 文件，格式与 geometry_data 一致</mark> 

<mark>Args:</mark> 

145 



<!-- Start of picture text -->
file_name: 文件名前缀<br>"""<br>if not self.pruned_network or self.pruned_network.number_of_edges() == 0:<br>print("没有可保存的路径网络")<br>return<br># 1. 提取 segments（直接使用网络的边）<br>segments = []<br>for edge in self.pruned_network.edges():<br>segment = [edge[0], edge[1]]<br>segments.append(segment)<br># 2. 生成 all_points（在线段上每 5 米取点）<br>all_points = []<br>point_interval = 5000 # 5 米 = 5000mm<br>min_end_distance = 10000 # 距离端点最小 10 米 = 10000mm<br>for edge in self.pruned_network.edges():<br>start_point = edge[0]<br>end_point = edge[1]<br># 计算线段长度<br>segment_length = np.sqrt((end_point[0] - start_point[0])**2 +<br>(end_point[1] - start_point[1])**2)<br># 修改这里：如果线段太短，根据长度决定取点策略<br>if segment_length < point_interval: # 小于 5 米<br># 只添加起点，避免两点距离小于 5 米<br>all_points.append(start_point)<br>continue<br>elif segment_length <= min_end_distance: # 5-10 米之间<br># 添加起点和终点（距离>=5 米）<br>all_points.append(start_point)<br>all_points.append(end_point)<br>continue<br># 计算单位方向向量<br>direction_x = (end_point[0] - start_point[0]) / segment_length<br>direction_y = (end_point[1] - start_point[1]) / segment_length<br># 添加起点<br>all_points.append(start_point)<br># 从起点开始每 5 米取一个点<br>current_distance = point_interval<br>while current_distance <= segment_length - min_end_distance:<br>point_x = start_point[0] + current_distance * direction_x<br>point_y = start_point[1] + current_distance * direction_y<br>all_points.append((point_x, point_y))<br>current_distance += point_interval<br># 添加终点<br>all_points.append(end_point)<br># 3. 去除重复点<br>unique_all_points = []<br>tolerance = 1.0 # 1mm 容差<br>for point in all_points:<br>is_duplicate = False<br>for existing_point in unique_all_points:<br>distance = np.sqrt((point[0] - existing_point[0])**2 +<br>(point[1] - existing_point[1])**2)<br>if distance < tolerance:<br>is_duplicate = True<br>break<br>if not is_duplicate:<br>unique_all_points.append(point)<br># 4. 转换为 Excel 格式<br>segments_data = []<br>all_points_data = []<br><!-- End of picture text -->

146

# 处理 segments - 每个 segment 作为一个分组<br>for i, segment in enumerate(segments):<br># 添加分组标识符<br>segments_data.append(f"{{0; {i}}}")<br># 添加 segment 中的点<br>for j, point in enumerate(segment):<br>segments_data.append(f"{j+1}. {{{point[0]}, {point[1]}, 0}}")<br># 处理 all_points<br>for point in unique_all_points:<br>all_points_data.append(f"{{{point[0]}, {point[1]}, 0}}")<br># 5. 创建 DataFrame 并保存<br>import pandas as pd<br># 确保两列长度一致<br>max_length = max(len(segments_data), len(all_points_data))<br># 填充较短的列<br>while len(segments_data) < max_length:<br>segments_data.append("")<br>while len(all_points_data) < max_length:<br>all_points_data.append("")<br># 创建 DataFrame<br>df = pd.DataFrame({<br>'区分线段的坐标': segments_data,<br>'不区分线段的坐标': all_points_data<br>})<br># 保存到 Excel<br>excel_filename = f"{file_name}-路网络.xlsx"<br>with pd.ExcelWriter(excel_filename, engine='openpyxl') as writer:<br>df.to_excel(writer, sheet_name='路网络', index=False)<br>print(f"路径网络已保存到: {excel_filename}")<br>return excel_filename<br><!-- End of picture text -->

<mark>''' 园林景观网格扫描建模系统 采用射线投射算法 栅格数据的生成与加载 ''' import numpy as np import pandas as pd import pickle import json from collections import defaultdict, Counter from shapely.geometry import Point, LineString, Polygon from rtree import index import matplotlib.pyplot as plt import matplotlib.patches as patches from matplotlib.patches import Circle from concurrent.futures import ProcessPoolExecutor, as_completed from multiprocessing import cpu_count import warnings import os warnings.filterwarnings('ignore') # 设置中文字体 plt.rcParams['font.family'] = ['simhei'] plt.rcParams['axes.unicode_minus'] = False # 导入已有模块</mark>

<mark>from load_and_visualize import NetworkLoader, NetworkVisualizer def compute_block_grid_matrix(args): """ 独立的进程计算函数 - 计算单个分割块的网格扫描矩阵 """ block_id, block_center, scan_area_size, analyzer_params, segments_info_data = args # 在进程内重建分析器状态 analyzer = BlockGridAnalyzer( scan_area_size=scan_area_size, grid_resolution=analyzer_params['grid_resolution'] ) # 重建 segments_info 和空间索引 analyzer.segments_info = segments_info_data # 重建空间索引 from rtree import index spatial_idx = index.Index() for i, segment_info in enumerate(analyzer.segments_info): spatial_idx.insert(i, segment_info['geometry'].bounds) analyzer.spatial_index = spatial_idx # 计算网格扫描矩阵（包含二次填充） grid_matrix = analyzer.compute_grid_matrix(block_center) occlusion_ratio = analyzer.compute_occlusion_ratio(grid_matrix) return block_id, { 'block_center': block_center, 'scan_area_size': scan_area_size, 'grid_matrix': grid_matrix, 'occlusion_ratio': occlusion_ratio } class BlockGridAnalyzer: """分割块网格扫描分析器""" def __init__(self, scan_area_size=10000, grid_resolution=1000): """ Args: scan_area_size: 扫描区域边长（毫米）10 米 = 10,000mm grid_resolution: 网格分辨率（毫米/像素）1 米 = 1,000mm/像素 """ self.scan_area_size = scan_area_size self.grid_resolution = grid_resolution self.grid_size = int(scan_area_size / grid_resolution) # 网格尺寸（像素） # 景观元素编号映射 self.element_mapping = { '被遮挡': 0, '半开放建筑': 1, '实体建筑': 2, '道路': 3, '假山': 4, '水体': 5, '植物': 6, '空旷区域': 7 # 没有障碍物的区域 } # 遮挡性元素（只有实体建筑会完全遮挡视线） self.occlusion_elements = {'实体建筑'} print(f"初始化网格扫描分析器: {self.grid_size}×{self.grid_size}网格, 分辨率 {grid_resolution}mm/pixel") def build_spatial_index(self, geometry_data): """构建空间索引""" print("构建空间索引...") # 创建 R-tree 空间索引 spatial_idx = index.Index() # 存储线段信息</mark> 

148 

<mark>self.segments_info = [] segment_id = 0 for element_type, data in geometry_data.items(): if element_type == "植物": # 植物处理为圆形边界 for plant in data: center = plant['center'] radius = plant['radius'] # 简化为正八边形近似圆 angles = np.linspace(0, 2*np.pi, 9)[:-1] # 8 个顶点 vertices = [(center[0] + radius * np.cos(a), center[1] + radius * np.sin(a)) for a in angles] # 创建边界线段 for i in range(len(vertices)): start = vertices[i] end = vertices[(i + 1) % len(vertices)] line = LineString([start, end]) # 添加到索引 spatial_idx.insert(segment_id, line.bounds) self.segments_info.append({ 'id': segment_id, 'element_type': element_type, 'geometry': line }) segment_id += 1 else: # 处理线段数据 for segment in data.get('segments', []): if len(segment) > 1: for i in range(len(segment) - 1): start = segment[i] end = segment[i + 1] line = LineString([start, end]) # 添加到索引 spatial_idx.insert(segment_id, line.bounds) self.segments_info.append({ 'id': segment_id, 'element_type': element_type, 'geometry': line }) segment_id += 1 self.spatial_index = spatial_idx print(f"空间索引构建完成: {len(self.segments_info)} 个线段") return spatial_idx def cast_ray_to_point_improved(self, observer_point, target_point): """ 改进的射线投射算法：基于交点分段填充 实现逻辑： 1. 从观测点向目标点发射射线 2. 找到所有与射线相交的线段，按距离排序 3. 根据交点位置进行分段填充： - 从观测点到第一个交点：标记为第一个交点的类型 - 第一个交点到第二个交点：标记为第二个交点的类型 - 以此类推 4. 如果目标点超出了所有交点，延长射线寻找下一个交点 5. 如果没有找到交点，标记为空旷区域</mark> 

<mark>Args:</mark> 

149 



<!-- Start of picture text -->
observer_point: 观察点坐标 (x, y)<br>target_point: 目标点坐标 (x, y)<br>Returns:<br>int: 景观元素编号<br>"""<br>target_distance = np.sqrt((target_point[0] - observer_point[0])**2 +<br>(target_point[1] - observer_point[1])**2)<br>if target_distance < 1: # 距离太近，直接返回空旷区域<br>return self.element_mapping['空旷区域']<br># 计算射线方向<br>direction_x = (target_point[0] - observer_point[0]) / target_distance<br>direction_y = (target_point[1] - observer_point[1]) / target_distance<br># 1. 构建基础射线（从观测点到目标点）<br>base_ray = LineString([observer_point, target_point])<br># 2. 找到所有与基础射线相交的线段<br>intersections = self._find_ray_intersections(base_ray, observer_point)<br># 3. 如果完全没有交点，直接标记为空旷区域，不延长射线<br>if not intersections:<br>return self.element_mapping['空旷区域']<br># 4. 根据交点位置确定目标点的景观类型<br>return self._determine_point_type_by_intersections(<br>intersections, target_distance, observer_point, direction_x, direction_y<br>)<br>def _find_ray_intersections(self, ray, observer_point):<br>"""<br>找到射线与所有线段的交点<br>Args:<br>ray: 射线 LineString 对象<br>observer_point: 观察点坐标<br>Returns:<br>list: 交点信息列表，每个元素包含距离、类型、坐标<br>"""<br>intersections = []<br>for seg_id in self.spatial_index.intersection(ray.bounds):<br>segment_info = self.segments_info[seg_id]<br>intersection = ray.intersection(segment_info['geometry'])<br>if not intersection.is_empty:<br># 处理交点坐标<br>intersection_points = []<br>if hasattr(intersection, 'coords'): # Point<br>intersection_points = [list(intersection.coords)[0]]<br>elif hasattr(intersection, 'geoms'): # MultiPoint 或其他几何体集合<br>for geom in intersection.geoms:<br>if hasattr(geom, 'coords'):<br>intersection_points.extend(list(geom.coords))<br># 计算每个交点的距离并添加到结果中<br>for point_coords in intersection_points:<br>distance = np.sqrt((point_coords[0] - observer_point[0])**2 +<br>(point_coords[1] - observer_point[1])**2)<br>if distance > 1: # 忽略太近的点（避免数值误差）<br>intersections.append({<br>'distance': distance,<br>'element_type': segment_info['element_type'],<br>'point': point_coords,<br>'segment_id': seg_id<br>})<br># 按距离排序<br><!-- End of picture text -->

<!-- Start of picture text -->
intersections.sort(key=lambda x: x['distance'])<br># 去除重复的交点（距离相近的认为是同一个点）<br>filtered_intersections = []<br>for intersection in intersections:<br>if not filtered_intersections or \<br>abs(intersection['distance'] - filtered_intersections[-1]['distance']) > 10:<br>filtered_intersections.append(intersection)<br>return filtered_intersections<br>def _determine_point_type_by_intersections(self, intersections, target_distance,<br>observer_point, direction_x, direction_y):<br>"""<br>根据交点信息确定目标点的景观类型<br>Args:<br>intersections: 交点信息列表<br>target_distance: 目标点距离<br>observer_point: 观察点坐标<br>direction_x, direction_y: 射线方向<br>Returns:<br>int: 景观元素编号<br>"""<br>if not intersections:<br># 完全没有交点，标记为空旷区域<br>return self.element_mapping['空旷区域']<br># 情况 1：目标点在第一个交点之前<br>if target_distance <= intersections[0]['distance']:<br>return self.element_mapping.get(intersections[0]['element_type'],<br>self.element_mapping['空旷区域'])<br># 情况 2：目标点在相邻交点之间<br>for i in range(len(intersections) - 1):<br>if intersections[i]['distance'] < target_distance <= intersections[i +<br>1]['distance']:<br># 在两个交点之间，使用后一个交点的类型<br>return self.element_mapping.get(intersections[i + 1]['element_type'],<br>self.element_mapping['空旷区域'])<br># 情况 3：目标点在所有已知交点之后<br># 延长射线寻找下一个交点<br>extended_distance = max(target_distance * 2, intersections[-1]['distance'] * 1.5)<br>extended_point = (<br>observer_point[0] + direction_x * extended_distance,<br>observer_point[1] + direction_y * extended_distance<br>)<br>extended_ray = LineString([observer_point, extended_point])<br># 寻找延长部分的交点<br>extended_intersections = self._find_ray_intersections(extended_ray, observer_point)<br># 筛选出目标点之后的交点<br>future_intersections = [<br>inter for inter in extended_intersections<br>if inter['distance'] > target_distance<br>]<br>if future_intersections:<br># 找到目标点之后的交点，向内填充<br>return self.element_mapping.get(future_intersections[0]['element_type'],<br>self.element_mapping['空旷区域'])<br>else:<br># 没有找到更远的交点，使用最后已知交点的类型<br>return self.element_mapping.get(intersections[-1]['element_type'],<br>self.element_mapping['空旷区域'])<br>def horizontal_line_scan_walking(self, observer_point, y_coord, x_start, x_end,<br><!-- End of picture text -->

direction='left_to_right'):<br>"""<br>沿边界行走的水平线扫描（确保返回长度一致）<br>"""<br># 预检查：构建整行的射线，看是否有任何交点<br>if direction == 'left_to_right':<br>end_point = (x_end, y_coord)<br>else: # right_to_left<br>end_point = (x_start, y_coord)<br>full_line_ray = LineString([observer_point, end_point])<br>line_intersections = self._find_ray_intersections(full_line_ray, observer_point)<br># 计算预期的网格点数量（确保一致性）<br>expected_grid_count = int(abs(x_end - x_start) / self.grid_resolution) + 1<br># 如果整行完全没有交点，直接返回全部为'空旷区域'的结果<br>if not line_intersections:<br>return [self.element_mapping['空旷区域']] * expected_grid_count<br># 使用固定步长生成坐标，确保数量一致<br>if direction == 'left_to_right':<br>x_coords = [x_start + i * self.grid_resolution for i in range(expected_grid_count)]<br># 确保最后一个点不超过 x_end<br>if x_coords[-1] > x_end:<br>x_coords[-1] = x_end<br>else: # right_to_left<br>x_coords = [x_end - i * self.grid_resolution for i in range(expected_grid_count)]<br># 确保最后一个点不低于 x_start<br>if x_coords[-1] < x_start:<br>x_coords[-1] = x_start<br>scan_line_result = []<br>for x_coord in x_coords:<br>target_point = (x_coord, y_coord)<br># 使用改进的射线投射算法，从当前观测点到目标点<br>element_code = self.cast_ray_to_point_improved(observer_point, target_point)<br>scan_line_result.append(element_code)<br>if direction == 'right_to_left':<br>scan_line_result.reverse()<br># 确保返回的结果长度正确<br>if len(scan_line_result) != expected_grid_count:<br>print(f"警告：扫描结果长度不匹配，预期{expected_grid_count}，实际<br>{len(scan_line_result)}")<br># 修正长度<br>if len(scan_line_result) < expected_grid_count:<br># 用最后一个值填充<br>scan_line_result.extend([scan_line_result[-1]] * (expected_grid_count -<br>len(scan_line_result)))<br>else:<br># 截断到预期长度<br>scan_line_result = scan_line_result[:expected_grid_count]<br>return scan_line_result<br>def _process_walking_occlusion(self, left_to_right_result, right_to_left_result):<br>"""<br>行走模式的遮挡处理<br>逻辑：<br>1. 基础结果以左到右扫描为准<br>2. 如果右到左扫描在某些位置看到了不同的景观元素，进行合理融合<br>3. 只有当左右都看到实体建筑，且中间有空隙时，才标记为被遮挡<br>Args:<br>left_to_right_result: 从左边界向右的扫描结果<br>right_to_left_result: 从右边界向左的扫描结果<br><!-- End of picture text -->
