<mark>Returns: list: 融合后的扫描结果 """ final_result = left_to_right_result.copy() # 找到左侧扫描遇到的第一个实体建筑位置 left_building_pos = -1 for i, val in enumerate(left_to_right_result): if val == self.element_mapping['实体建筑']: left_building_pos = i break # 找到右侧扫描遇到的第一个实体建筑位置 right_building_pos = -1 for i, val in enumerate(right_to_left_result): if val == self.element_mapping['实体建筑']: right_building_pos = len(right_to_left_result) - 1 - i # 转换为左到右的索引 break # 情况 1：左右都有实体建筑，且中间有空隙 if (left_building_pos != -1 and right_building_pos != -1 and left_building_pos < right_building_pos): # 中间区域的处理：结合左右观察结果 for i in range(left_building_pos + 1, right_building_pos): left_view = final_result[i] right_view = right_to_left_result[len(right_to_left_result) - 1 - i] # 如果左边看到的是空旷区域，但右边看到了景观元素，采用右边的结果 if (left_view == self.element_mapping['空旷区域'] and right_view != self.element_mapping['空旷区域'] and right_view != self.element_mapping['被遮挡']): final_result[i] = right_view # 如果左右都是空旷区域，标记为被遮挡 elif (left_view == self.element_mapping['空旷区域'] and right_view == self.element_mapping['空旷区域']): final_result[i] = self.element_mapping['被遮挡'] # 情况 2：只有左边有实体建筑，右边补充信息 elif left_building_pos != -1 and right_building_pos == -1: # 实体建筑后面的区域，尝试用右边的观察结果补充 for i in range(left_building_pos + 1, len(final_result)): if final_result[i] == self.element_mapping['空旷区域']: right_view = right_to_left_result[len(right_to_left_result) - 1 - i] if right_view != self.element_mapping['空旷区域']: final_result[i] = right_view return final_result def _process_walking_occlusion_fill_blanks(self, left_to_right_result, right_to_left_result): """ 专门用于填充空白的行走模式遮挡处理</mark> 

<mark>逻辑： 1. 基础结果以左到右扫描为准 2. 只有当左到右扫描结果为空旷区域时，才用右到左的结果替换 3. 其他情况保持左到右的结果不变 Args: left_to_right_result: 从左边界向右的扫描结果 right_to_left_result: 从右边界向左的扫描结果 Returns: list: 融合后的扫描结果 """ final_result = left_to_right_result.copy()</mark> 

153 

<mark># 遍历每个位置 for i in range(len(final_result)): # 只有当左到右扫描结果为空旷区域时，才考虑用右到左的结果替换 if final_result[i] == self.element_mapping['空旷区域']: # 获取对应的右到左扫描结果 right_index = len(right_to_left_result) - 1 - i if 0 <= right_index < len(right_to_left_result): right_view = right_to_left_result[right_index] # 如果右到左扫描看到了有效的景观元素（不是空旷区域），就用它填充 if right_view != self.element_mapping['空旷区域']: final_result[i] = right_view return final_result def compute_grid_matrix(self, block_center): """ 计算分割块的网格扫描矩阵（沿边界行走扫描模式，确保矩阵形状一致） """ # 定义分割块边界 half_size = self.scan_area_size / 2 min_x = block_center[0] - half_size max_x = block_center[0] + half_size min_y = block_center[1] - half_size max_y = block_center[1] + half_size grid_matrix = [] # 预计算预期的矩阵尺寸 expected_rows = int(self.scan_area_size / self.grid_resolution) expected_cols = int(self.scan_area_size / self.grid_resolution) print(f" 预期矩阵尺寸: {expected_rows}×{expected_cols}") # 逐行扫描（从上到下） y_coords = np.arange(max_y, min_y - self.grid_resolution, -self.grid_resolution) # 确保 y_coords 的数量与预期行数一致 if len(y_coords) != expected_rows: # 重新生成确保数量正确 y_coords = [max_y - i * self.grid_resolution for i in range(expected_rows)] for i, y_coord in enumerate(y_coords): # 当前行的观测点位于左边界 current_observer = (min_x, y_coord) # 从左边界向右扫描 left_to_right_result = self.horizontal_line_scan_walking( current_observer, y_coord, min_x, max_x, 'left_to_right' ) # 检查扫描结果长度 if len(left_to_right_result) != expected_cols: #print(f" 警告：第{i}行扫描长度不匹配，预期{expected_cols}，实际 {len(left_to_right_result)}") # 修正长度 if len(left_to_right_result) < expected_cols: left_to_right_result.extend([left_to_right_result[-1]] * (expected_cols - len(left_to_right_result))) else: left_to_right_result = left_to_right_result[:expected_cols] # 检查是否有空旷区域，如果是，从右边界补充扫描 has_open_area = self.element_mapping['空旷区域'] in left_to_right_result if has_open_area: # 从右边界向左补充扫描 right_observer = (max_x, y_coord) right_to_left_result = self.horizontal_line_scan_walking( right_observer, y_coord, min_x, max_x, 'right_to_left' ) # 确保右侧扫描结果长度也正确</mark> 

154 

<mark>if len(right_to_left_result) != expected_cols: if len(right_to_left_result) < expected_cols: right_to_left_result.extend([right_to_left_result[-1]] * (expected_cols - len(right_to_left_result))) else: right_to_left_result = right_to_left_result[:expected_cols] # 处理遮挡：只对空白区域进行填充 final_result = self._process_walking_occlusion_fill_blanks( left_to_right_result, right_to_left_result ) else: # 没有空旷区域，直接使用左到右的结果 final_result = left_to_right_result # 最终确保长度正确 if len(final_result) != expected_cols: if len(final_result) < expected_cols: final_result.extend([final_result[-1]] * (expected_cols - len(final_result))) else: final_result = final_result[:expected_cols] grid_matrix.append(final_result) # 转换为 numpy 数组前最后检查 print(f" 实际矩阵行数: {len(grid_matrix)}") if grid_matrix: row_lengths = [len(row) for row in grid_matrix] print(f" 各行长度: 最小{min(row_lengths)}, 最大{max(row_lengths)}, 预期 {expected_cols}") # 如果仍有长度不一致的行，进行最后修正 for i, row in enumerate(grid_matrix): if len(row) != expected_cols: print(f" 修正第{i}行长度: {len(row)} -> {expected_cols}") if len(row) < expected_cols: grid_matrix[i] = row + [row[-1]] * (expected_cols - len(row)) else: grid_matrix[i] = row[:expected_cols] try: grid_matrix = np.array(grid_matrix, dtype=int) print(f" 成功创建矩阵: {grid_matrix.shape}") except ValueError as e: print(f" 创建 numpy 数组失败: {e}") print(f" 尝试强制修正...") # 强制修正：确保所有行都有相同长度 if grid_matrix: target_length = expected_cols corrected_matrix = [] for i, row in enumerate(grid_matrix): if len(row) == target_length: corrected_matrix.append(row) elif len(row) < target_length: corrected_row = row + [self.element_mapping['空旷区域']] * (target_length - len(row)) corrected_matrix.append(corrected_row) else: corrected_row = row[:target_length] corrected_matrix.append(corrected_row) grid_matrix = np.array(corrected_matrix, dtype=int) print(f" 修正后矩阵形状: {grid_matrix.shape}") # 添加二次填充步骤 print(f"开始二次填充空白区域...") filled_matrix = self.secondary_fill_empty_areas(grid_matrix, block_center)</mark>

<mark>return filled_matrix def _process_occlusion_improved(self, left_to_right_result, right_to_left_result, observer_point, y_coord, min_x, max_x): """ 保留此方法以确保向后兼容，但在新的行走模式中不使用 """ return self._process_walking_occlusion(left_to_right_result, right_to_left_result) def compute_occlusion_ratio(self, grid_matrix): """计算被遮挡比例""" total_elements = grid_matrix.size occluded_elements = np.sum(grid_matrix == self.element_mapping['被遮挡']) return occluded_elements / total_elements if total_elements > 0 else 0 def secondary_fill_empty_areas(self, grid_matrix, block_center): """ 二次填充空白区域 算法逻辑： 1. 对空白区域，从该位置发射四个方向的射线 2. 找到每个方向上第一个相交的线段类型 3. 优先级：左右相同 > 上下相同 > 四个方向最近的 Args: grid_matrix: 原始网格扫描矩阵 block_center: 分割块中心点（用于坐标转换） Returns: numpy.ndarray: 二次填充后的网格矩阵 """ filled_matrix = grid_matrix.copy() rows, cols = filled_matrix.shape empty_area_code = self.element_mapping['空旷区域'] # 计算分割块边界 half_size = self.scan_area_size / 2 min_x = block_center[0] - half_size max_y = block_center[1] + half_size # 找到所有空白区域的位置 empty_positions = [] for i in range(rows): for j in range(cols): if filled_matrix[i, j] == empty_area_code: empty_positions.append((i, j)) if not empty_positions: return filled_matrix print(f"发现 {len(empty_positions)} 个空白位置需要二次填充") # 对每个空白位置进行填充 filled_count = 0 for row, col in empty_positions: # 将网格坐标转换为世界坐标 world_x = min_x + col * self.grid_resolution world_y = max_y - row * self.grid_resolution world_point = (world_x, world_y) fill_value = self._determine_secondary_fill_by_raycast(world_point, empty_area_code) if fill_value != empty_area_code: filled_matrix[row, col] = fill_value filled_count += 1 print(f"二次填充完成: {filled_count}/{len(empty_positions)} 个位置被填充") return filled_matrix def _determine_secondary_fill_by_raycast(self, world_point, empty_code): """</mark> 

156 

<mark>通过射线投射确定空白位置的填充值 Args: world_point: 世界坐标系中的空白位置 empty_code: 空白区域编码 Returns: int: 填充值 """ # 定义四个方向的射线方向向量和扩展距离 directions = { 'left': (-1, 0), 'right': (1, 0), 'up': (0, 1), 'down': (0, -1) } # 射线最大扩展距离 max_extension = max(self.scan_area_size * 2, 50000) # 确保能覆盖整个区域 # 对每个方向发射射线，找到第一个相交的线段 intersection_results = {} for direction_name, (dx, dy) in directions.items(): # 创建射线终点 end_point = ( world_point[0] + dx * max_extension, world_point[1] + dy * max_extension ) # 发射射线并找到第一个相交的线段 intersected_segment = self._cast_ray_find_first_intersection(world_point, end_point) if intersected_segment is not None: intersection_results[direction_name] = intersected_segment # 按优先级规则确定填充值 return self._apply_priority_rules(intersection_results, empty_code) def _cast_ray_find_first_intersection(self, start_point, end_point): """ 发射射线找到第一个相交的线段类型 Args: start_point: 射线起点 end_point: 射线终点 Returns: str or None: 第一个相交的线段类型，如果没有相交则返回 None """ from shapely.geometry import LineString ray = LineString([start_point, end_point]) closest_intersection = None closest_distance = float('inf') closest_element_type = None # 在空间索引中查找可能相交的线段 for seg_id in self.spatial_index.intersection(ray.bounds): segment_info = self.segments_info[seg_id] intersection = ray.intersection(segment_info['geometry']) if not intersection.is_empty: # 处理交点坐标 intersection_points = [] if hasattr(intersection, 'coords'): # Point intersection_points = [list(intersection.coords)[0]] elif hasattr(intersection, 'geoms'): # MultiPoint 或其他几何体集合 for</mark> <u><mark>geom</mark></u> <mark>in intersection.geoms:</mark>

if hasattr(geom, 'coords'):<br>intersection_points.extend(list(geom.coords))<br># 找到最近的交点<br>for point_coords in intersection_points:<br>distance = np.sqrt((point_coords[0] - start_point[0])**2 +<br>(point_coords[1] - start_point[1])**2)<br>if distance > 1 and distance < closest_distance: # 忽略太近的点<br>closest_distance = distance<br>closest_element_type = segment_info['element_type']<br>closest_intersection = point_coords<br>return closest_element_type<br>def _apply_priority_rules(self, intersection_results, empty_code):<br>"""<br>应用优先级规则确定填充值<br>优先级：<br>1. 左右射线相交同一类型线段<br>2. 上下射线相交同一类型线段<br>3. 四个方向最近的线段<br>4. 保持空白<br>Args:<br>intersection_results: 各方向射线的相交结果<br>empty_code: 空白区域编码<br>Returns:<br>int: 填充值<br>"""<br>if not intersection_results:<br>return empty_code<br># 规则 1：检查左右是否相交同一类型线段<br>if ('left' in intersection_results and 'right' in intersection_results and<br>intersection_results['left'] == intersection_results['right']):<br>return self.element_mapping.get(intersection_results['left'], empty_code)<br># 规则 2：检查上下是否相交同一类型线段<br>if ('up' in intersection_results and 'down' in intersection_results and<br>intersection_results['up'] == intersection_results['down']):<br>return self.element_mapping.get(intersection_results['up'], empty_code)<br># 规则 3：四个方向最常见的线段类型<br>if intersection_results:<br>segment_counts = {}<br>for segment_type in intersection_results.values():<br>segment_counts[segment_type] = segment_counts.get(segment_type, 0) + 1<br># 返回出现次数最多的景观类型<br>most_common_segment = max(segment_counts.items(), key=lambda x: x[1])[0]<br>return self.element_mapping.get(most_common_segment, empty_code)<br># 规则 4：保持空白<br>return empty_code<br>def _prepare_process_args(self):<br>"""准备进程计算所需的参数"""<br>analyzer_params = {<br>'grid_resolution': self.grid_resolution<br>}<br># 准备 segments_info 数据（需要可序列化）<br>segments_info_data = []<br>for segment_info in self.segments_info:<br>segments_info_data.append({<br>'id': segment_info['id'],<br>'element_type': segment_info['element_type'],<br><!-- End of picture text -->

'geometry': segment_info['geometry']<br>})<br>return analyzer_params, segments_info_data<br>class GardenBlockModeler:<br>"""园林分割块建模系统"""<br>def __init__(self, block_size=200000, overlap_distance=50000, occlusion_threshold=0.5):<br>"""<br>Args:<br>block_size: 分割块大小（毫米）200 米 = 200,000mm<br>overlap_distance: 重叠距离（毫米）50 米 = 50,000mm<br>occlusion_threshold: 遮挡阈值<br>"""<br>self.block_size = block_size<br>self.overlap_distance = overlap_distance<br>self.occlusion_threshold = occlusion_threshold<br># 实际步长（扣除重叠）<br>self.step_size = block_size - overlap_distance<br># 初始化加载器<br>self.loader = NetworkLoader()<br>self.visualizer = NetworkVisualizer(self.loader)<br>print(f"分割块大小: {block_size}mm, 重叠距离: {overlap_distance}mm, 实际步长:<br>{self.step_size}mm")<br>def main(file_name):<br>"""主函数"""<br># 文件路径<br>original_excel = f"shujv/4-{file_name}数据坐标.xlsx"<br>print(f"=== 园林网格扫描建模(改进版): {file_name} ===")<br># 创建建模系统（手动设置参数）<br>modeler = GardenBlockModeler(<br>block_size=10000, # 分割块大小 10 米<br>overlap_distance=0, # 重叠距离 2 米<br>occlusion_threshold=0.5 # 遮挡率阈值 50%<br>)<br>#略<br>if __name__ == "__main__":<br># 使用示例 "寄畅园", "留园", "耦园", "绮园", "秋霞圃", "沈园","怡园", "豫园","瞻园" , "拙<br>政园"<br>file_names = ["近园"]<br>for file_name in file_names:<br>valid_blocks, grid_results, garden_bounds = main(file_name)<br>garden_illusion_evaluation.py<br>'''<br>园林幻境感量化评价系统<br>基于景观生态学指标和空间形态学方法实现园林幻境感的定量评价<br>主要功能:<br>1. 数据处理与分析框架<br>2. 元素分布特征分析（基于景观生态学指标）<br>3. 开合变化空间分析（基于空间形态学方法）<br>4. 幻境感综合评价体系<br>5. 结果保存与可视化接口<br>'''<br>class GardenIllusionEvaluator:<br>"""园林幻境感量化评价器"""<br>def __init__(self, baseline_garden_name: str = None):<br>"""<br><!-- End of picture text -->

<!-- Start of picture text -->
Args:<br>baseline_garden_name: 基准园林名称，用于标准化评分<br>"""<br>self.baseline_garden_name = baseline_garden_name<br>self.baseline_metrics = None # 基准园林的指标值<br># 元素编码映射（与 GardenBlockModeler 保持一致）<br>self.element_mapping = {<br>'被遮挡': 0,<br>'半开放建筑': 1,<br>'实体建筑': 2,<br>'道路': 3,<br>'假山': 4,<br>'水体': 5,<br>'植物': 6,<br>'空旷区域': 7<br>}<br># 实体元素（用于开合分析）<br>self.solid_elements = {0, 1, 2, 4} # 被遮挡、半开放建筑、实体建筑、假山<br>self.open_elements = {3, 5, 6, 7} # 道路、水体、植物、空旷区域<br># 评价指标权重配置<br>self.weights = {<br># 元素分布维度 (50%)<br>'shannon_diversity': 0.10, # Shannon 多样性指数<br>'aggregation_index': 0.15, # 聚集度指数<br>'contagion_index': 0.10, # 蔓延度指数<br>'edge_density': 0.10, # 边缘密度指数<br>'moran_i': 0.05, # Moran's I 指数<br># 开合变化维度 (50%)<br>'mean_nearest_neighbor': 0.15, # 平均最近邻距离<br>'enclosure_ratio': 0.15, # 围合比指数<br>'connectivity_index': 0.10, # 连通性指数<br>'compactness_index': 0.05, # 紧凑度指数<br>'integral_connectivity': 0.05 # 整体连通性指数<br>}<br>print(f"初始化园林幻境感评价系统")<br>print(f"基准园林: {baseline_garden_name or '未设置'}")<br>def combine_valid_blocks_to_matrix(self, all_blocks: List, grid_results: Dict) -><br>np.ndarray:<br>"""将所有有效分割块的网格矩阵拼接成完整园林矩阵"""<br>print("开始拼接有效分割块...")<br># 过滤有效块<br>valid_blocks = [block for block in all_blocks if block.get('is_valid', False)]<br>if not valid_blocks:<br>print("没有有效的分割块")<br>return None<br>print(f"找到 {len(valid_blocks)} 个有效分割块")<br># 计算整体边界<br>min_row = min(block['row'] for block in valid_blocks)<br>max_row = max(block['row'] for block in valid_blocks)<br>min_col = min(block['col'] for block in valid_blocks)<br>max_col = max(block['col'] for block in valid_blocks)<br># 假设每个网格矩阵大小为 10x10<br>grid_size = 10 # 1000mm 分辨率下 10m 分割块对应 10x10 网格<br># 计算完整矩阵尺寸<br>total_rows = (max_row - min_row + 1) * grid_size<br>total_cols = (max_col - min_col + 1) * grid_size<br># 创建完整矩阵（初始化为空旷区域）<br>combined_matrix = np.full((total_rows, total_cols), 7, dtype=int)<br>print(f"完整矩阵尺寸: {total_rows}x{total_cols}")<br><!-- End of picture text -->

<mark># 填充各个有效块的数据 filled_count = 0 for block in valid_blocks: block_id = block['id'] if block_id not in grid_results: continue result = grid_results[block_id] block_matrix = result['grid_matrix'] # 计算该块在完整矩阵中的位置 row_offset = (block['row'] - min_row) * grid_size col_offset = (block['col'] - min_col) * grid_size # 确保不超出边界 max_rows = min(row_offset + block_matrix.shape[0], total_rows) max_cols = min(col_offset + block_matrix.shape[1], total_cols) # 填充数据 combined_matrix[row_offset:max_rows, col_offset:max_cols] = \ block_matrix[:max_rows-row_offset, :max_cols-col_offset] filled_count += 1 print(f"成功拼接 {filled_count} 个有效分割块的网格矩阵") return combined_matrix def identify_connected_components(self, matrix: np.ndarray) -> Dict[int, List]: """识别连通域（patches）""" print("识别连通域...") patches = {} # 对每个元素类型识别连通域 for element_type in range(8): # 创建二值掩码 binary_mask = (matrix == element_type) if np.sum(binary_mask) == 0: patches[element_type] = [] continue # 使用 4 邻接识别连通域 labeled_array, num_features = ndimage.label(binary_mask, structure=np.ones((3,3))) patch_list = [] for i in range(1, num_features + 1): patch_coords = np.where(labeled_array == i) patch_size = len(patch_coords[0]) # 计算边界信息 rows, cols = patch_coords min_row, max_row = np.min(rows), np.max(rows) min_col, max_col = np.min(cols), np.max(cols) # 计算周长（边界像素数） perimeter = self._calculate_perimeter(labeled_array == i) patch_info = { 'id': i, 'size': patch_size, 'coords': patch_coords, 'bbox': (min_row, min_col, max_row, max_col), 'perimeter': perimeter } patch_list.append(patch_info) patches[element_type] = patch_list total_patches = sum(len(patch_list) for patch_list in patches.values()) print(f"连通域识别完成: 共 {total_patches} 个 patches") return patches def _calculate_perimeter(self, binary_mask: np.ndarray) -> int: """计算二值图像的周长""" # 使用 Sobel 算子计算边缘 edges = ndimage.binary_erosion(binary_mask) ^ binary_mask</mark> 

161 

<mark>return np.sum(edges) def compute_shannon_diversity_index(self, matrix: np.ndarray) -> float: """计算 Shannon 多样性指数""" # 统计各元素类型的面积占比 total_area = matrix.size element_counts = np.bincount(matrix.flatten(), minlength=8) proportions = element_counts / total_area # 计算 Shannon 指数：SHDI = -∑(Pi × ln Pi) shdi = 0.0 for p in proportions: if p > 0: shdi -= p * np.log(p) return shdi def compute_largest_patch_index(self, patches: Dict[int, List]) -> float: """计算最大斑块指数""" total_area = sum( sum(patch['size'] for patch in patch_list) for patch_list in patches.values() ) if total_area == 0: return 0.0 # 找到最大斑块 max_patch_size = 0 for patch_list in patches.values(): for patch in patch_list: max_patch_size = max(max_patch_size, patch['size']) # LPI = max(aij)/A × 100 lpi = (max_patch_size / total_area) * 100 return lpi def compute_aggregation_index(self, matrix: np.ndarray, patches: Dict[int, List]) -> float: """计算聚集度指数""" # AI = [∑(gii)/(max→gii)] × 100 total_internal_adjacencies = 0 total_max_adjacencies = 0 for element_type in range(8): element_mask = (matrix == element_type) element_count = np.sum(element_mask) if element_count == 0: continue # 计算内部邻接数（4 邻接） internal_adjacencies = 0 rows, cols = np.where(element_mask) for r, c in zip(rows, cols): # 检查 4 个邻接方向 for dr, dc in [(-1, 0), (1, 0), (0, -1), (0, 1)]: nr, nc = r + dr, c + dc if (0 <= nr < matrix.shape[0] and 0 <= nc < matrix.shape[1] and matrix[nr, nc] == element_type): internal_adjacencies += 1 # 最大可能的内部邻接数 max_adjacencies = 2 * element_count * (element_count - 1) total_internal_adjacencies += internal_adjacencies total_max_adjacencies += max_adjacencies if total_max_adjacencies == 0: return 0.0 ai = (total_internal_adjacencies / total_max_adjacencies) * 100 return ai def compute_contagion_index(self, matrix: np.ndarray) -> float: """计算蔓延度指数"""</mark> 

<mark># CONTAG =</mark> <u><mark>[1</mark></u> <mark>+</mark> <u><mark>∑∑(Pi</mark></u> <mark>×</mark> <u><mark>gik/∑gik</mark></u> <mark>× ln(Pi ×</mark> <u><mark>gik/∑gik))/(2×ln(m))]</mark></u> <mark>× 100</mark>

<mark># 统计各元素类型的占比 element_counts = np.bincount(matrix.flatten(), minlength=8) total_pixels = matrix.size proportions = element_counts / total_pixels # 统计相邻关系 adjacency_matrix = np.zeros((8, 8)) for i in range(matrix.shape[0]): for j in range(matrix.shape[1]): current_type = matrix[i, j] # 检查右邻和下邻（避免重复计算） for di, dj in [(0, 1), (1, 0)]: ni, nj = i + di, j + dj if ni < matrix.shape[0] and nj < matrix.shape[1]: neighbor_type = matrix[ni, nj] adjacency_matrix[current_type, neighbor_type] += 1 adjacency_matrix[neighbor_type, current_type] += 1 # 计算蔓延度指数 m = 8 # 元素类型数量 total_adjacencies = np.sum(adjacency_matrix) if total_adjacencies == 0: return 0.0 contagion_sum = 0.0 for i in range(8): if proportions[i] == 0: continue for k in range(8): if adjacency_matrix[i, k] == 0: continue gik = adjacency_matrix[i, k] sum_gik = np.sum(adjacency_matrix[i, :]) if sum_gik > 0: term = proportions[i] * (gik / sum_gik) * np.log(proportions[i] * (gik / sum_gik)) contagion_sum += term contag = (1 + contagion_sum / (2 * np.log(m))) * 100 return contag def compute_edge_density(self, matrix: np.ndarray) -> float: """计算边缘密度指数""" # ED = E/A × 10000 total_edge_length = 0 # 计算所有相邻像素间的边缘 for i in range(matrix.shape[0]): for j in range(matrix.shape[1]): current_type = matrix[i, j] # 检查右邻和下邻 for di, dj in [(0, 1), (1, 0)]: ni, nj = i + di, j + dj if ni < matrix.shape[0] and nj < matrix.shape[1]: neighbor_type = matrix[ni, nj] if current_type != neighbor_type: total_edge_length += 1 total_area = matrix.size ed = (total_edge_length / total_area) * 10000 return ed def compute_moran_i(self, matrix: np.ndarray) -> float: """计算 Moran's I 空间自相关指数""" # I = n∑∑wij(xi-x</mark> ̄ <mark>)(xj-x</mark> ̄ <mark>)/[(∑∑wij)∑(xi-x</mark> ̄ <mark>)²] rows, cols = matrix.shape n = rows * cols # 展平矩阵</mark> 

163 

<mark>flat_matrix = matrix.flatten() mean_value = np.mean(flat_matrix) # 计算空间权重和空间自相关 numerator = 0.0 denominator = 0.0 total_weights = 0.0 for i in range(rows): for j in range(cols): idx1 = i * cols + j x1 = flat_matrix[idx1] # 计算与均值的偏差 dev1 = x1 - mean_value denominator += dev1 * dev1 # 4 邻接权重 for di, dj in [(-1, 0), (1, 0), (0, -1), (0, 1)]: ni, nj = i + di, j + dj if 0 <= ni < rows and 0 <= nj < cols: idx2 = ni * cols + nj x2 = flat_matrix[idx2] dev2 = x2 - mean_value weight = 1.0 # 邻接权重为 1 numerator += weight * dev1 * dev2 total_weights += weight if denominator == 0 or total_weights == 0: return 0.0 moran_i = (n * numerator) / (total_weights * denominator) return moran_i def compute_mean_nearest_neighbor_distance(self, matrix: np.ndarray) -> float: """计算平均最近邻距离""" # 提取开放性元素的坐标 open_coords = [] for element_type in self.open_elements: coords = np.where(matrix == element_type) if len(coords[0]) > 0: open_coords.extend(list(zip(coords[0], coords[1]))) # 提取实体元素的坐标 solid_coords = [] for element_type in self.solid_elements: coords = np.where(matrix == element_type) if len(coords[0]) > 0: solid_coords.extend(list(zip(coords[0], coords[1]))) if not open_coords or not solid_coords: return 0.0 # 计算每个开放元素到最近实体元素的距离 open_array = np.array(open_coords) solid_array = np.array(solid_coords) distances = cdist(open_array, solid_array) min_distances = np.min(distances, axis=1) mean_nearest_neighbor = np.mean(min_distances) return mean_nearest_neighbor def compute_enclosure_ratio(self, matrix: np.ndarray) -> float: """计算围合比指数""" # ER = Le/Lp，其中 Le 为被实体元素围合的边界长度，Lp 为总边界长度 total_boundary_length = 0 enclosed_boundary_length = 0 # 扫描所有边界像素 for i in range(matrix.shape[0]): for j in range(matrix.shape[1]): current_type = matrix[i, j] # 检查是否为边界像素（与不同类型相邻）</mark> 

164 

<mark>is_boundary = False adjacent_to_solid = False for di, dj in [(-1, 0), (1, 0), (0, -1), (0, 1)]: ni, nj = i + di, j + dj if 0 <= ni < matrix.shape[0] and 0 <= nj < matrix.shape[1]: neighbor_type = matrix[ni, nj] if current_type != neighbor_type: is_boundary = True if neighbor_type in self.solid_elements: adjacent_to_solid = True</mark> 

<mark>if is_boundary:</mark> 

<mark>total_boundary_length += 1 if adjacent_to_solid: enclosed_boundary_length += 1 if total_boundary_length == 0: return 0.0 enclosure_ratio = enclosed_boundary_length / total_boundary_length return enclosure_ratio def compute_connectivity_index(self, patches: Dict[int, List]) -> float: """计算连通性指数""" # CONNECT = [∑cij/n(n-1)/2] × 100 total_connections = 0 total_possible_connections = 0 for element_type, patch_list in patches.items(): n = len(patch_list) if n <= 1: continue # 计算同类型斑块间的连接数（简化处理：距离小于阈值认为连通） connections = 0 threshold = 5.0 # 连通阈值 for i in range(n): for j in range(i + 1, n):</mark> 

<mark>patch1 = patch_list[i] patch2 = patch_list[j] # 计算斑块中心距离 center1 = self._get_patch_center(patch1) center2 = self._get_patch_center(patch2) distance = np.sqrt((center1[0] - center2[0])**2 + (center1[1] - center2[1])**2) if distance <= threshold: connections += 1 total_connections += connections total_possible_connections += n * (n - 1) // 2 if total_possible_connections == 0: return 0.0</mark> 

<mark>connectivity = (total_connections / total_possible_connections) * 100 return connectivity def _get_patch_center(self, patch: Dict) -> Tuple[float, float]: """计算斑块中心坐标""" coords = patch['coords'] center_row = np.mean(coords[0]) center_col = np.mean(coords[1]) return (center_row, center_col) def compute_compactness_index(self, patches: Dict[int, List]) -> float: """计算紧凑度指数""" # CI = A/P²，其中 A 为面积，P 为周长 total_compactness = 0.0 total_patches = 0 for patch_list in patches.values(): for</mark> <u><mark>patch</mark></u> <mark>in</mark> <u><mark>patch_list:</mark></u> 

165 

<mark>area = patch['size'] perimeter = patch['perimeter'] if perimeter > 0: compactness = area / (perimeter * perimeter) total_compactness += compactness total_patches += 1 if total_patches == 0: return 0.0 # 返回平均紧凑度 avg_compactness = total_compactness / total_patches return avg_compactness def compute_integral_connectivity(self, patches: Dict[int, List]) -> float: """计算整体连通性指数""" # IIC = ∑∑(ai×aj)/(1+nlij)/AL² # 简化实现：基于斑块面积和距离的连通性度量 all_patches = [] for patch_list in patches.values(): all_patches.extend(patch_list) if len(all_patches) <= 1: return 0.0 total_area = sum(patch['size'] for patch in all_patches) iic_sum = 0.0 for i, patch1 in enumerate(all_patches): for j, patch2 in enumerate(all_patches): if i == j: continue area1 = patch1['size'] area2 = patch2['size'] # 计算距离（简化为斑块中心距离） center1 = self._get_patch_center(patch1) center2 = self._get_patch_center(patch2) distance = np.sqrt((center1[0] - center2[0])**2 + (center1[1] - center2[1])**2) # 连接步数（简化处理） connection_steps = max(1, int(distance / 10)) # 假设 10 个像素为一步 iic_sum += (area1 * area2) / (1 + connection_steps) if total_area == 0: return 0.0 iic = iic_sum / (total_area * total_area) return iic def evaluate_garden_complete(self, garden_name: str, original_excel: str = None) -> Dict: """完整的园林幻境感评价流程""" print(f"\n{'='*60}") print(f"开始园林幻境感完整评价: {garden_name}") print(f"{'='*60}") # 拼接完整园林矩阵 combined_matrix = self.combine_valid_blocks_to_matrix(all_blocks, grid_results) if combined_matrix is None: print("错误: 无法拼接园林矩阵") return None # 计算所有评价指标 metrics = self.compute_all_metrics(combined_matrix) # 标准化得分 normalized_scores = self.normalize_metrics(metrics) # 计算幻境感得分 illusion_scores = self.compute_illusion_score(normalized_scores) # 保存结果 save_dir = self.save_evaluation_results( garden_name, metrics, normalized_scores, illusion_scores, combined_matrix.shape</mark> <u><mark>)</mark></u>

<mark>""" 栅格级园林特征提取器 在 10×10 矩阵级别计算定义特征并与图像特征合并 主要功能： 1. 在单个 10×10 矩阵上计算 10 个定义的园林特征 2. 加载 64 维深度图像特征 3. 合并成 74 维综合特征向量 4. 批量处理所有分割块并存储结果 """ class BlockLevelMetricsCalculator: """分割块级别的园林指标计算器""" def __init__(self): """初始化计算器""" # 元素编码映射（与 GardenBlockModeler 保持一致） self.element_mapping = { '被遮挡': 0, '半开放建筑': 1, '实体建筑': 2, '道路': 3, '假山': 4, '水体': 5, '植物': 6, '空旷区域': 7 } # 实体元素（用于开合分析） self.solid_elements = {0, 1, 2, 4} # 被遮挡、半开放建筑、实体建筑、假山 self.open_elements = {3, 5, 6, 7} # 道路、水体、植物、空旷区域 def identify_connected_components(self, matrix: np.ndarray) -> Dict[int, List]: """识别 10×10 矩阵中的连通域（patches）""" def _calculate_perimeter(self, binary_mask: np.ndarray) -> int: """计算二值图像的周长""" # 与问题二计算相同 class BlockFeatureExtractor: """分割块级别的综合特征提取器""" def __init__(self, use_image_features=True): """ 初始化特征提取器 Args: use_image_features: 是否使用 64 维图像特征 """ self.use_image_features = use_image_features and FEATURE_EXTRACTOR_AVAILABLE self.metrics_calculator = BlockLevelMetricsCalculator() # 特征维度配置 self.defined_feature_dim = 10 # 定义特征维度 self.image_feature_dim = 64 # 图像特征维度 self.total_feature_dim = self.defined_feature_dim + (self.image_feature_dim if self.use_image_features else 0)</mark>

专门用于学习园林 10×10 矩阵的深度特征表示<br>主要功能：<br>1. 构建卷积自编码器进行无监督特征学习<br>2. 从园林空间矩阵中提取高维语义特征<br>3. 保存训练好的模型和特征向量<br>栅栏矩阵的空间结构特征提取<br>"""<br>class GardenFeatureExtractor:<br>"""园林空间深度特征提取器"""<br>def __init__(self, input_shape=(10, 10, 8), latent_dim=64,<br>model_name="garden_autoencoder"):<br>"""<br>初始化特征提取器<br>Args:<br>input_shape: 输入形状 (高度, 宽度, 通道数)<br>latent_dim: 特征向量维度<br>model_name: 模型名称<br>"""<br>self.input_shape = input_shape<br>self.latent_dim = latent_dim<br>self.model_name = model_name<br># 模型组件<br>self.encoder = None<br>self.decoder = None<br>self.autoencoder = None<br># 训练历史<br>self.training_history = None<br># 元素映射<br>self.element_mapping = {<br>'被遮挡': 0, '半开放建筑': 1, '实体建筑': 2, '道路': 3,<br>'假山': 4, '水体': 5, '植物': 6, '空旷区域': 7<br>}<br>self.element_names = ['被遮挡', '半开放建筑', '实体建筑', '道路',<br>'假山', '水体', '植物', '空旷区域']<br>print(f"园林特征提取器初始化完成")<br>print(f"输入形状: {input_shape}, 特征维度: {latent_dim}")<br>def build_encoder(self):<br>"""<br>构建编码器网络<br>专注于捕获园林空间的层次化特征<br>"""<br>print("构建编码器网络...")<br>inputs = keras.Input(shape=self.input_shape, name='encoder_input')<br># 第一层：捕获局部空间模式<br>x = layers.Conv2D(64, (3, 3), activation='relu', padding='same',<br>name='conv1')(inputs)<br>x = layers.BatchNormalization(name='bn1')(x)<br>x = layers.Dropout(0.1)(x)<br># 第二层：学习中等复杂度的空间关系<br>x = layers.Conv2D(128, (3, 3), activation='relu', padding='same',<br>name='conv2')(x)<br>x = layers.BatchNormalization(name='bn2')(x)<br>x = layers.Dropout(0.1)(x)<br># 第三层：捕获复杂的空间布局模式<br>x = layers.Conv2D(256, (3, 3), activation='relu', padding='same',<br>name='conv3')(x)<br>x = layers.BatchNormalization(name='bn3')(x)<br>x = layers.Dropout(0.1)(x)<br># 第四层：深度特征抽象<br><!-- End of picture text -->

<mark>encoded = self.encoder(inputs) decoded = self.decoder(encoded) self.autoencoder = Model(inputs, decoded, name='autoencoder') # 编译模型 self.autoencoder.compile( optimizer=keras.optimizers.Adam(learning_rate=0.001), loss='categorical_crossentropy', metrics=['accuracy'] ) return self.autoencoder def train(self, X, validation_split=0.2, epochs=100, batch_size=32, early_stopping_patience=15): """ 训练自编码器 Args: X: 训练数据 validation_split: 验证集比例 epochs: 训练轮数 batch_size: 批大小 early_stopping_patience: 早停耐心值 Returns: training_history: 训练历史 """ # 准备回调函数 callbacks_list = [ callbacks.EarlyStopping( monitor='val_loss', patience=early_stopping_patience, restore_best_weights=True, verbose=1 ), callbacks.ReduceLROnPlateau( monitor='val_loss', factor=0.5, patience=8, min_lr=1e-7, verbose=1 ), callbacks.ModelCheckpoint( filepath=f'checkpoints/{self.model_name}_best.h5', monitor='val_loss', save_best_only=True, save_weights_only=False, verbose=1 ) ] # 创建检查点目录 os.makedirs('checkpoints', exist_ok=True) # 开始训练 start_time = time.time() history = self.autoencoder.fit( X, X, # 自编码器的目标是重构输入 validation_split=validation_split, epochs=epochs, batch_size=batch_size, callbacks=callbacks_list, verbose=1, shuffle=True</mark>

garden_similarity_analyzer.py<br>"""<br>江南古典园林异同分析系统<br>基于 179 维特征向量对园林进行多维度相似性分析、共性特征识别、个性化评估<br>主要功能：<br>1. 多层次相似度计算（专家特征、网络特征、空间特征、抽象特征）<br>2. 共有美学特征识别与稳定性分析<br>3. 园林个性化差异量化<br>4. 聚类分析与类型识别<br>5. 美学规律量化表达<br>6. 综合可视化与分析报告<br>特征向量结构（179 维）：<br>- 微观特征：第 1-148 维（栅格聚合特征：均值 74 维+标准差 74 维）<br>- 中观特征：第 149-164 维（空间关系 16 维）<br>- 宏观特征：第 165-179 维（专家特征 10 维+网络特征 5 维）<br>"""<br>class GardenSimilarityAnalyzer:<br>"""江南古典园林异同分析器"""<br>def __init__(self):<br>"""初始化分析器"""<br>self.vector_builder = GardenVectorBuilder()<br>self.output_dir = "GardenAnalysis"<br># 确保输出目录存在<br>if not os.path.exists(self.output_dir):<br>os.makedirs(self.output_dir)<br>print(f"创建分析输出目录: {self.output_dir}")<br># 特征维度配置<br>self.micro_range = (0, 148) # 微观特征：1-148 维（索引 0-147）<br>self.meso_range = (148, 164) # 中观特征：149-164 维（索引 148-163）<br>self.macro_expert_range = (164, 174) # 专家特征：165-174 维（索引 164-173）<br>self.macro_network_range = (174, 179) # 网络特征：175-179 维（索引 174-178）<br># 特征名称配置<br>self.expert_feature_names = [<br>'shannon_diversity', 'aggregation_index', 'contagion_index',<br><!-- End of picture text -->

<mark>'edge_density', 'moran_i', 'mean_nearest_neighbor', 'enclosure_ratio', 'connectivity_index', 'compactness_index', 'integral_connectivity' ] self.network_feature_names = [ 'network_node_count', 'network_edge_count', 'network_avg_degree', 'network_max_degree', 'network_degree_std' ] self.meso_feature_names = [ 'adjacent_similarity_mean', 'adjacent_similarity_std', 'gradient_east', 'gradient_west', 'gradient_south', 'gradient_north', 'pca_component_1', 'pca_component_2', 'pca_component_3', 'cluster_proportion_1', 'cluster_proportion_2', 'cluster_proportion_3', 'variance_mean', 'skewness_mean', 'kurtosis_mean', 'convex_hull_log_volume' ] # 分析结果存储 self.garden_data = None self.similarity_results = {} self.commonality_results = {} self.personality_results = {} self.clustering_results = {} def analyze_similarity(self) -> Dict[str, Any]: """ 多层次相似度分析 Returns: Dict: 相似度分析结果 """ garden_names = self.garden_data['success_gardens'] n_gardens = len(garden_names) # 构建向量矩阵 vectors = np.array([self.garden_data['garden_vectors'][name]['vector'] for name in garden_names]) print(f"分析 {n_gardens} 个园林的相似性") print(f"向量维度: {vectors.shape}") # 1. 专家特征相似度（165-174 维，索引 164-173） expert_vectors = vectors[:, self.macro_expert_range[0]:self.macro_expert_range[1]] expert_similarity = cosine_similarity(expert_vectors) print(f"专家特征相似度计算完成 - 基于{expert_vectors.shape[1]}维特征") # 2. 网络特征相似度（175-179 维，索引 174-178） network_vectors = vectors[:, self.macro_network_range[0]:self.macro_network_range[1]] network_similarity = cosine_similarity(network_vectors) print(f"网络特征相似度计算完成 - 基于{network_vectors.shape[1]}维特征") # 3. 空间关系相似度（149-164 维，索引 148-163） spatial_vectors = vectors[:, self.meso_range[0]:self.meso_range[1]] spatial_similarity = cosine_similarity(spatial_vectors) print(f"空间关系相似度计算完成 - 基于{spatial_vectors.shape[1]}维特征") # 4. 抽象特征整体相似度（1-148 维，索引 0-147） abstract_vectors = vectors[:, self.micro_range[0]:self.micro_range[1]] abstract_similarity = cosine_similarity(abstract_vectors) print(f"抽象特征相似度计算完成 - 基于{abstract_vectors.shape[1]}维特征") # 5. 综合相似度（加权平均） # 权重设计：专家特征 40%（美学评价最重要）、网络 20%、空间 20%、抽象 20% comprehensive_similarity = (0.4 * expert_similarity + 0.2 * network_similarity + 0.2 * spatial_similarity + 0.2 * abstract_similarity) # 6. 相似度统计分析 similarity_stats = self._analyze_similarity_statistics(</mark>

garden_vector_builder.py<br>"""<br>园林多尺度多模态特征向量构建器<br>实现园林的 179 维特征向量计算，包含微观、中观、宏观三个尺度的特征<br>特征体系：<br>- 微观尺度（148 维）：栅格聚合特征（均值 74 维 + 标准差 74 维）<br>- 中观尺度（16 维）：空间关系特征（6 维）+ 点云分布特征（10 维）<br>- 宏观尺度（15 维）：专家定义特征（10 维）+ 网络结构特征（5 维）<br>总维度：179 维<br>"""<br>class GardenVectorBuilder:<br>"""园林多尺度多模态特征向量构建器"""<br>def __init__(self):<br>"""初始化向量构建器"""<br>self.data_loader = MultiSourceDataLoader()<br>self.output_dir = "GardenVector"<br># 确保输出目录存在<br>if not os.path.exists(self.output_dir):<br>os.makedirs(self.output_dir)<br>print(f"创建输出目录: {self.output_dir}")<br># 特征维度配置<br>self.micro_dim = 148 # 微观特征维度<br>self.meso_dim = 16 # 中观特征维度<br>self.macro_dim = 15 # 宏观特征维度<br><!-- End of picture text -->

174 

<mark>self.total_dim = 179 # 总维度 def build_garden_vectors(self, garden_names: List[str]) -> Dict[str, Any]: """ 为指定园林列表构建多尺度多模态特征向量 Args: garden_names: 园林名称列表 Returns: Dict: 包含所有园林向量和元数据的字典 """ print(f"=== 开始构建园林向量 ===") print(f"目标园林: {garden_names}") # 1. 加载多源数据 print("\n1. 加载多源数据...") data_results = self.data_loader.load_all_data(garden_names) if not data_results['loaded_gardens']: print("错误: 没有成功加载任何园林数据") return None # 筛选栅格级数据 if data_results['block_level_data']: filtered_block_data = self.data_loader.filter_block_data_by_gardens( data_results['block_level_data'], data_results['loaded_gardens']) data_results['block_level_data'] = filtered_block_data # 2. 为每个园林构建特征向量 print(f"\n2. 构建特征向量...") garden_vectors = {} success_gardens = [] failed_gardens = [] for garden_name in data_results['loaded_gardens']: print(f"\n 处理园林: {garden_name}") try: # 构建单个园林的特征向量 vector_result = self._build_single_garden_vector( garden_name, data_results) if vector_result: garden_vectors[garden_name] = vector_result success_gardens.append(garden_name) print(f" ✓ 向量构建成功: {len(vector_result['vector'])}维") else: failed_gardens.append(garden_name) print(f" ✗ 向量构建失败") except Exception as e: failed_gardens.append(garden_name) print(f" ✗ 向量构建异常: {e}") # 3. 整理结果 results = { 'garden_vectors': garden_vectors, 'success_gardens': success_gardens, 'failed_gardens': failed_gardens, 'vector_config': { 'total_dim': self.total_dim, 'micro_dim': self.micro_dim, 'meso_dim': self.meso_dim, 'macro_dim': self.macro_dim, 'feature_names': self._get_feature_names() }, 'data_summary': self.data_loader.get_data_summary(data_results) }</mark> <u><mark>print(f"\n===</mark></u> <mark>向量构建完成 ===")</mark> 

175 

<mark>print(f"成功构建: {len(success_gardens)} 个园林向量") print(f"构建失败: {len(failed_gardens)} 个园林向量") if failed_gardens: print(f"失败园林: {failed_gardens}") # 4. 保存结果 if success_gardens: self._save_garden_vectors(results) return results def _build_single_garden_vector(self, garden_name: str, data_results: Dict) -> Optional[Dict]: """ 构建单个园林的 179 维特征向量 Args: garden_name: 园林名称 data_results: 多源数据结果 Returns: Dict: 包含向量和详细信息的字典 """ try: # 获取该园林的数据 network_data = data_results['network_data'].get(garden_name) garden_level_data = data_results['garden_level_data'].get(garden_name) block_level_data = data_results['block_level_data'] # 获取该园林的栅格数据 garden_block_indices = [] garden_block_positions = [] for i, meta in enumerate(block_level_data['metadata']): if meta.get('garden_name') == garden_name: garden_block_indices.append(i) # 获取栅格位置（中心坐标） center = meta.get('center', (0, 0)) garden_block_positions.append(center) # 提取该园林的栅格特征矩阵 garden_grid_features = block_level_data['features'][garden_block_indices] # (N, 74) garden_grid_positions = np.array(garden_block_positions) # (N, 2) # 1. 提取微观特征（148 维） micro_features = self._extract_micro_features(garden_grid_features) # 2. 提取中观特征（16 维） meso_features = self._extract_meso_features(garden_grid_features, garden_grid_positions) # 3. 提取宏观特征（15 维） macro_features = self._extract_macro_features(garden_level_data, network_data) # 4. 组合为完整向量 complete_vector = np.concatenate([micro_features, meso_features, macro_features]) # 5. 构建多模态特征划分（这个有点鸡肋） multimodal_features = self._extract_multimodal_features( micro_features, meso_features, macro_features) # 验证维度 assert len(complete_vector) == self.total_dim, f"向量维度错误: {len(complete_vector)} != {self.total_dim}" assert len(micro_features) == self.micro_dim, f"微观特征维度错误: {len(micro_features)} != {self.micro_dim}" assert len(meso_features) == self.meso_dim, f"中观特征维度错误: {len(meso_features)} != {self.meso_dim}" assert len(macro_features) == self.macro_dim, f"宏观特征维度错误: {len(macro_features)} != {self.macro_dim}" vector_result =</mark> <u><mark>{</mark></u> 

176 

<mark>'vector': complete_vector, # 完整的 179 维向量 'micro_features': micro_features, # 148 维微观特征 'meso_features': meso_features, # 16 维中观特征 'macro_features': macro_features, # 15 维宏观特征 'multimodal_features': multimodal_features, # 多模态特征划分 'metadata': { 'garden_name': garden_name, 'vector_dim': len(complete_vector), 'grid_count': len(garden_block_indices), 'network_nodes': network_data['node_count'], 'network_edges': network_data['edge_count'], 'total_score': garden_level_data['total_score'] } } return vector_result except Exception as e: print(f" 异常: 构建向量失败 - {e}") return None def _extract_micro_features(self, grid_features: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray: """ 提取微观尺度特征（栅格聚合特征）- 148 维 Args: grid_features: 栅格特征矩阵 (N, 74) Returns: np.ndarray: 148 维微观特征 = 均值 74 维 + 标准差 74 维 """ # 计算 74 维特征在所有栅格上的均值和标准差 mean_features = np.mean(grid_features, axis=0) # (74,) std_features = np.std(grid_features, axis=0) # (74,) # 拼接为 148 维特征 micro_features = np.concatenate([mean_features, std_features]) # (148,) return micro_features def _extract_meso_features(self, grid_features: np.ndarray, grid_positions: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray: """ 提取中观尺度特征（空间关系特征）- 16 维 Args: grid_features: 栅格特征矩阵 (N, 74) grid_positions: 栅格位置矩阵 (N, 2) Returns: np.ndarray: 16 维中观特征 = 基础空间关系 6 维 + 点云分布特征 10 维 """ features = [] # ===== 基础空间关系特征（6 维）===== # 1. 相邻相似度统计（2 维） adjacent_similarities = self._compute_adjacent_similarities(grid_features, grid_positions) if len(adjacent_similarities) > 0: features.extend([np.mean(adjacent_similarities), np.std(adjacent_similarities)]) else: features.extend([0.0, 0.0]) # 如果没有相邻栅格，使用默认值 # 2. 方向梯度特征（4 维） directional_gradients = self._compute_directional_gradients(grid_features,</mark> <u><mark>grid_positions)</mark></u>

<!-- Start of picture text -->
features.extend(directional_gradients) # [east, west, south, north]<br># ===== 点云分布特征（10 维）=====<br># 3. PCA 主成分（3 维）<br>pca_features = self._compute_pca_features(grid_features)<br>features.extend(pca_features)<br># 4. 聚类特征（3 维）<br>cluster_features = self._compute_cluster_features(grid_features)<br>features.extend(cluster_features)<br># 5. 分布形状（3 维）<br>shape_features = self._compute_shape_features(grid_features)<br>features.extend(shape_features)<br># 6. 离散度（1 维）<br>dispersion_feature = self._compute_dispersion_feature(grid_positions)<br>features.append(dispersion_feature)<br>meso_features = np.array(features) # (16,)<br>return meso_features<br>def _extract_macro_features(self, garden_level_data: Dict, network_data: Dict) -><br>np.ndarray:<br>"""<br>提取宏观尺度特征（整体特征）- 15 维<br>Args:<br>garden_level_data: 园林级评估数据<br>network_data: 游园网络数据<br>Returns:<br>np.ndarray: 15 维宏观特征 = 专家定义特征 10 维 + 网络结构特征 5 维<br>"""<br>features = []<br># ===== 专家定义特征（10 维）=====<br>feature_names = garden_level_data['feature_names'] # 10 个特征名称<br>normalized_scores = garden_level_data['normalized_scores']<br># 按顺序提取 10 个专家特征<br>for feature_name in feature_names:<br>score = normalized_scores.get(feature_name, 0.0)<br>features.append(score)<br># ===== 网络结构特征（5 维）=====<br>graph = network_data['macro_graph']<br># 1. 节点数<br>node_count = graph.number_of_nodes()<br>features.append(node_count)<br># 2. 边数<br>edge_count = graph.number_of_edges()<br>features.append(edge_count)<br># 3. 平均度<br>if node_count > 0:<br>degrees = [d for n, d in graph.degree()]<br>avg_degree = np.mean(degrees) if degrees else 0.0<br>max_degree = max(degrees) if degrees else 0.0<br>std_degree = np.std(degrees) if len(degrees) > 1 else 0.0<br>else:<br>avg_degree = 0.0<br>max_degree = 0.0<br>std_degree = 0.0<br>features.append(avg_degree)<br># 4. 最大度中心性<br>features.append(max_degree)<br># 5. 度分布标准差<br>features.append(std_degree)<br><!-- End of picture text -->

macro_features = np.array(features) # (15,)<br>return macro_features<br>def _extract_multimodal_features(self, micro_features: np.ndarray,<br>meso_features: np.ndarray,<br>macro_features: np.ndarray) -> Dict[str, np.ndarray]:<br>"""<br>提取多模态特征划分<br>根据园林特征的语义含义重新划分为三种模态：<br>- 几何空间模态：空间关系、形状、位置等几何特征<br>- 景观语义模态：景观类型、美学评价、功能性等语义特征<br>- 尺度层次模态：不同尺度的统计特征<br>Returns:<br>Dict: 包含三种模态特征的字典<br>"""<br># ===== 几何空间模态（53 维）=====<br># 包含：空间关系、几何形状、网络拓扑等几何相关特征<br>geometric_components = []<br># 1. 栅格空间分布的几何统计（20 维）<br># 选择与空间分布相关的前 20 个均值特征<br>geometric_components.append(micro_features[:20]) # 20 维<br># 2. 栅格空间分布的几何变异（20 维）<br># 选择对应的前 20 个标准差特征<br>geometric_components.append(micro_features[74:94]) # 20 维<br># 3. 中观空间关系特征（8 维）<br># 相邻相似度(2 维) + 方向梯度(4 维) + 离散度(1 维) + PCA 第一主成分(1 维)<br>spatial_features = np.concatenate([<br>meso_features[:6], # 相邻相似度 + 方向梯度，6 维<br>[meso_features[15]], # 离散度，1 维<br>[meso_features[6]] # PCA 第一主成分，1 维<br>])<br>geometric_components.append(spatial_features) # 8 维<br># 4. 网络几何结构特征（5 维）<br># 网络的拓扑结构特征<br>geometric_components.append(macro_features[10:15]) # 5 维<br>geometric_features = np.concatenate(geometric_components) # 20+20+8+5=53 维<br># ===== 景观语义模态（116 维）=====<br># 包含：景观类型、美学特征、功能评价等语义相关特征<br>semantic_components = []<br># 1. 栅格景观语义统计（54 维）<br># 选择与景观类型相关的后 54 个均值特征<br>semantic_components.append(micro_features[20:74]) # 54 维<br># 2. 栅格景观语义变异（54 维）<br># 选择对应的后 54 个标准差特征<br>semantic_components.append(micro_features[94:148]) # 54 维<br># 3. 专家美学评价特征（10 维）<br># 园林专家定义的美学和功能性评价指标<br>semantic_components.append(macro_features[:10]) # 10 维<br># 注意：116 = 54 + 54 + 10 = 118，需要调整<br># 实际取前 52 维 + 后 52 维 + 专家特征 10 维 = 114 维<br># 为了凑够 116 维，再加上 2 维 PCA 特征<br>semantic_components = []<br>semantic_components.append(micro_features[22:74]) # 52 维<br>semantic_components.append(micro_features[96:148]) # 52 维<br>semantic_components.append(macro_features[:10]) # 10 维<br>semantic_components.append(meso_features[7:9]) # PCA 第 2,3 主成分，2 维<br>semantic_features = np.concatenate(semantic_components) # 52+52+10+2=116 维<br># ===== 尺度层次模态（10 维）=====<br># 包含：不同尺度的代表性统计特征<br><!-- End of picture text -->

scale_components = []<br># 1. 微观尺度代表特征（4 维）<br># 选择具有代表性的微观特征：首尾均值和标准差<br>micro_repr = [<br>micro_features[0], # 第 1 个特征的均值<br>micro_features[36], # 第 37 个特征的均值<br>micro_features[74], # 第 1 个特征的标准差<br>micro_features[110] # 第 37 个特征的标准差<br>]<br>scale_components.extend(micro_repr) # 4 维<br># 2. 中观尺度代表特征（3 维）<br># 选择中观层面最重要的 3 个特征<br>meso_repr = [<br>meso_features[0], # 相邻相似度均值<br>meso_features[12], # 分布形状-方差均值<br>meso_features[15] # 离散度<br>]<br>scale_components.extend(meso_repr) # 3 维<br># 3. 宏观尺度代表特征（3 维）<br># 选择宏观层面最重要的 3 个特征<br>macro_repr = [<br>macro_features[0], # 第 1 个专家特征<br>macro_features[9], # 第 10 个专家特征<br>macro_features[12] # 网络平均度<br>]<br>scale_components.extend(macro_repr) # 3 维<br>scale_features = np.array(scale_components) # 4+3+3=10 维<br># 验证维度<br>assert len(geometric_features) == 53, f"几何空间模态维度错误:<br>{len(geometric_features)} != 53"<br>assert len(semantic_features) == 116, f"景观语义模态维度错误:<br>{len(semantic_features)} != 116"<br>assert len(scale_features) == 10, f"尺度层次模态维度错误: {len(scale_features)} != 10"<br>return {<br>'geometric_modal': geometric_features, # 几何空间模态 53 维<br>'semantic_modal': semantic_features, # 景观语义模态 116 维<br>'scale_modal': scale_features # 尺度层次模态 10 维<br>}<br># ===== 中观特征计算辅助函数 =====<br>def _compute_adjacent_similarities(self, grid_features: np.ndarray,<br>grid_positions: np.ndarray) -> List[float]:<br>"""计算相邻栅格特征相似度"""<br>if len(grid_positions) < 2:<br>return []<br># 计算位置距离矩阵<br>pos_distances = cdist(grid_positions, grid_positions, 'euclidean')<br># 找到每个栅格的最近邻（排除自己）<br>similarities = []<br>for i in range(len(grid_features)):<br># 找到最近的邻居<br>neighbor_distances = pos_distances[i].copy()<br>neighbor_distances[i] = np.inf # 排除自己<br>nearest_neighbor_idx = np.argmin(neighbor_distances)<br># 计算特征相似度（使用余弦相似度）<br>feature_i = grid_features[i]<br>feature_j = grid_features[nearest_neighbor_idx]<br># 避免除零错误<br>norm_i = np.linalg.norm(feature_i)<br>norm_j = np.linalg.norm(feature_j)<br>if norm_i > 0 and norm_j > 0:<br><!-- End of picture text -->

<mark>similarity = np.dot(feature_i, feature_j) / (norm_i * norm_j) similarities.append(similarity) return similarities def _compute_directional_gradients(self, grid_features: np.ndarray, grid_positions: np.ndarray) -> List[float]: """计算四个主方向的特征梯度均值""" if len(grid_positions) < 2: return [0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0] gradients = {'east': [], 'west': [], 'south': [], 'north': []} for i in range(len(grid_features)): x_i, y_i = grid_positions[i] feature_i = grid_features[i] # 找到四个方向的邻居 for j in range(len(grid_features)): if i == j: continue x_j, y_j = grid_positions[j] feature_j = grid_features[j] # 计算方向 dx = x_j - x_i dy = y_j - y_i if abs(dx) > abs(dy): # 主要是水平方向 if dx > 0: # 东 gradient = np.mean(np.abs(feature_j - feature_i)) gradients['east'].append(gradient) else: # 西 gradient = np.mean(np.abs(feature_j - feature_i)) gradients['west'].append(gradient) else: # 主要是垂直方向 if dy > 0: # 北 gradient = np.mean(np.abs(feature_j - feature_i)) gradients['north'].append(gradient) else: # 南 gradient = np.mean(np.abs(feature_j - feature_i)) gradients['south'].append(gradient) # 计算各方向梯度均值 result = [] for direction in ['east', 'west', 'south', 'north']: if gradients[direction]: result.append(np.mean(gradients[direction])) else: result.append(0.0) return result def _compute_pca_features(self, grid_features: np.ndarray) -> List[float]: """计算 PCA 主成分特征""" if len(grid_features) < 3: # PCA 需要足够的样本 return [0.0, 0.0, 0.0] try: pca = PCA(n_components=3) pca.fit(grid_features) # 返回前 3 个主成分的方差解释比例 return pca.explained_variance_ratio_.tolist() except: return [0.0, 0.0, 0.0] def _compute_cluster_features(self, grid_features: np.ndarray) -> List[float]: """计算 K-means 聚类特征""" if len(grid_features) < 3: return [0.0, 0.0, 1.0] # 如果样本太少，假设都在一个聚类 try:</mark> 

<mark>kmeans = KMeans(n_clusters=3, random_state=42, n_init=10)</mark>

labels = kmeans.fit_predict(grid_features)<br># 计算各聚类的大小比例<br>unique_labels, counts = np.unique(labels, return_counts=True)<br>proportions = counts / len(grid_features)<br># 确保返回 3 个值<br>result = [0.0, 0.0, 0.0]<br>for i, prop in enumerate(proportions):<br>if i < 3:<br>result[i] = prop<br>return result<br>except:<br>return [0.0, 0.0, 1.0]<br>def _compute_shape_features(self, grid_features: np.ndarray) -> List[float]:<br>"""计算分布形状特征"""<br>if len(grid_features) < 2:<br>return [0.0, 0.0, 0.0]<br># 计算整体方差、偏度、峰度的均值<br>variance_mean = np.mean(np.var(grid_features, axis=0))<br>try:<br>skew_mean = np.mean([skew(grid_features[:, i]) for i in<br>range(grid_features.shape[1])])<br>kurt_mean = np.mean([kurtosis(grid_features[:, i]) for i in<br>range(grid_features.shape[1])])<br>except:<br>skew_mean = 0.0<br>kurt_mean = 0.0<br>return [variance_mean, skew_mean, kurt_mean]<br>def _compute_dispersion_feature(self, grid_positions: np.ndarray) -> float:<br>"""计算点云凸包体积的对数值"""<br>if len(grid_positions) < 3:<br>return 0.0<br>try:<br>from scipy.spatial import ConvexHull<br>hull = ConvexHull(grid_positions)<br>volume = hull.volume<br># 避免 log(0)<br>return np.log(volume + 1e-10)<br>except:<br># 如果凸包计算失败，使用位置分布的方差作为替代<br>return np.log(np.var(grid_positions) + 1e-10)<br><!-- End of picture text -->
